How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Carnival Leader
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Carnival Leader]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Grants the player 25 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]]) every real-life day.
Location: [[Carnival]]
Coordinates: -89.5 71 11.5
Carnival Leader is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Carnival]] during the [[Carnival]] event. They explain the carnival to the player and give 25 [[Carnival Ticket]] every real-life day.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction None|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Welcome to The Carnival, player! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Here, you'll be able to partake in fun activities, earn Carnival Tokens, and purchase cool prizes! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Each day, I'll give you 25x Carnival Tickets, which will let you play the games. [NPC] Carnival Leader: You'll also gain Carnival Tickets for participating in different Events! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Here, I'll give you some to start things off.|
|First Interaction After Claiming Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Alright, you should be good to go! [NPC] Carnival Leader: If you have any questions, come find me. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Good luck, and have A Carnivalot of Fun™!|
|Subsequent Interactions None|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Nice to see you here again, <player>! [NPC] Carnival Leader: It's a new day, which means you get more Carnival Tickets!|
|Subsequent Interactions After Claiming Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Remember, if you have any questions, I'll be right here. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Good luck, and have A Carnivalot of Fun™!|
|Subsequent Interactions Interacting After Claimed Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Welcome to The Carnival, player! [NPC] Carnival Leader: You've already claimed your Carnival Tickets for today, but I'm happy to answer any questions you might have. Select an option: ➜ [I should be good, thanks.] ➜ [What sorts of games can I play?] ➜ [How can I get more Carnival Tickets?] ➜ [How do I earn Carnival Tokens?] ➜ [Where can I turn my Carnival Tokens in?]|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [I should be good, thanks.]|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Alrighty. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Good luck, and have A Carnivalot of Fun™!|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [What sorts of games can I play?]|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Currently, The Carnival offers 3 different games! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Behind me is Fruit Digging. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Try your luck in this sand-flinging adventure and win big! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Over there is Zombie Shootout. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Aim for a high score by killing the pesky Zombies walking around the arena! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Lastly, there's Catch a Fish, located by the mountain pond. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Catch some fish and have a relaxing time! [NPC] Carnival Leader: It's A Carnivalot of Fun™! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Any other questions? Select an option: ➜ [I should be good, thanks.] ➜ [How can I get more Carnival Tickets?] ➜ [How do I earn Carnival Tokens?] ➜ [Where can I turn my Carnival Tokens in?]|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [How can I get more Carnival Tickets?]|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Oh, that's easy! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Each day, I'll give you 25x Carnival Tickets. [NPC] Carnival Leader: You'll also gain Carnival Tickets for participating in different Events! [NPC] Carnival Leader: Any other questions? Select an option: ➜ [I should be good, thanks.] ➜ [What sorts of games can I play?] ➜ [How do I earn Carnival Tokens?] ➜ [Where can I turn my Carnival Tokens in?]|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [How do I earn Carnival Tokens?]|[NPC] Carnival Leader: By competing in Carnival games! [NPC] Carnival Leader: You'll be awarded 1 Token for every 100 points you earn. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Any other questions? Select an option: ➜ [I should be good, thanks.] ➜ [What sorts of games can I play?] ➜ [How can I get more Carnival Tickets?] ➜ [Where can I turn my Carnival Tokens in?]|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [Where can I turn my Carnival Tokens in?]|[NPC] Carnival Leader: Why, at the orange stand right over there! [NPC] Carnival Leader: You can exchange your Tokens for permanent buffs in different Events throughout SkyBlock, as well as some fun masks to play around with. [NPC] Carnival Leader: Any other questions? Select an option: ➜ [I should be good, thanks.] ➜ [What sorts of games can I play?] ➜ [How can I get more Carnival Tickets?] ➜ [How do I earn Carnival Tokens?]|

# Carnival Pirateman
Aliases: Carnival Pirate
Categories: Non-player characters
[Carnival Pirateman]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to play the Fruit Digging ([[Carnival]]) minigame in the [[Carnival]].
Location: [[Carnival]]
Coordinates: -107.0 73 28.0
Carnival Pirateman is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Carnival]] during the [[Carnival]] event. They let the player compete in the [[Carnival|Fruit Digging]] minigame.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Welcome to Fruit Digging, matey! [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: The rules of the game are as follows - I give ye a shovel, ye dig up the sand on the lookout fer fruit! [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: There's a bunch o' different fruits, each with differin' point values and abilities. [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Ye can RIGHT CLICK with yer shovel to swap between dowsing modes, which give ye a little info every time ye dig! [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Yer supposed to use that info to avoid them bombs and pick the best blocks to mine. [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: It might all sound a little confusin', but I bet ye'll get the hang of it if ye give it a try. [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: What do ye say? Select an option: [Aye aye, Captain!] [I'll find me a different game.]|
|Subsequent Interactions|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Ahoy there, matey! [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Would ye like to do some Fruit Digging? Select an option: 🡺 [Aye sure do!] 🡺 [Could ye tell me the rules again, Captain?] 🡺 [I'll set me sights elsewhere.]|
|Selecting randomized Green Dialogue Option; (e.g. [Aye sure do!]) with 5 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Here's yer shovel, then. [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Good luck, matey!|
|Selecting randomized Green Dialogue Option; (e.g. [Aye sure do!]) without; 5 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Sorry lad, but ye don't have 5x Carnival Tickets. Ye gotta pay to play! [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Ye'll have to get yer nutrients elsewhere.|
|Selecting [Could ye tell me the rules again, Captain?]|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: The rules are as follows - I give ye a shovel, ye dig up the sand on the lookout fer fruit! [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: There's a bunch o' different fruits, each with differin' point values and abilities. [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Ye can RIGHT CLICK with yer shovel to swap between dowsing modes, which give ye a little info every time ye dig! [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Yer supposed to use that info to avoid them bombs and pick the best blocks to mine. [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: With that said, are ye ready to play? Select an option: 🡺 [I be ready as the tide!] 🡺 [Avast, I've changed me mind...]|
|Selecting randomized Red Dialogue Option; (e.g. [I'll set me sights elsewhere.])|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: A swashbucklin' shame, if ye ask me... [NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Whenever ye be ready, I'll be waitin'|
|Selecting randomized Green Dialogue Option; (e.g. [Aye sure do!]) after the [[Carnival]] has ended|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Blimey! The Carnival is about to close. Ye'll gotta come back next time.|
|Selecting randomized Green Dialogue Option; (e.g. [Aye sure do!]) in a laggy server|[NPC] Carnival Pirateman: Arrr, this server be slower than a sea turtle in a storm! No chance we be playin' this game, matey. Sorry!|

# Carpenter
Categories: Non-player characters, Carpentry
[Carpenter]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Carpentry ([[Quests]]); Rooting for You ([[Quests]])
Description: Unlocks the [[Carpentry]] skill, after the player has given 64 (1 stack) [[Wool]].
Location: [[Foraging Camp]]
Coordinates: -137.5 74 -41.5
The Carpenter is an [[NPC]] located within the [[Foraging Camp]], as well as the [[The Garden|Garden]].
## Quests
The Carpentry asks the player to gather 64 [[Wool]] for them to unlock to the [[Carpentry]] skill.
Later, they appear in the [[The Garden|Garden]] to help the player unlock the [[Greenhouse]] with the [[Greenhouse Blueprint]]. Once unlocked, the Carpenter will appear there and allow players to view their unlocked [[Mutations]] and unlock more plots in the Greenhouse using [[Ethereal Vine|Ethereal Vines]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Village]] First Interaction|[NPC] Carpenter: Hi, [player]! Welcome to the Furniture Shop. [NPC] Carpenter: Sales are too good right now, I can't keep up with the demand! [NPC] Carpenter: Could you bring a stack of White Wool to help replenish my stock? [NPC] Carpenter: Sheep over in The Barn drop wool, but you can also purchase it from the Wool Weaver. [NPC] Carpenter: She lives in a house in the main plaza near the bank.|
|[[Village]] Without 64 [[Wool]]|[NPC] Carpenter: Come back with a stack of White Wool!|
|[[Village]] Giving 64 [[Wool]]|[NPC] Carpenter: Wow, thanks so much for the help! [NPC] Carpenter: Carpentry is my passion, I always love to teach others. [NPC] Carpenter: Here's the recipe for the Carpentry Table. You can place it in your world and craft furniture that you've unlocked! [NPC] Carpenter: You can now gain Carpentry XP by crafting items. Leveling your Carpentry Skill unlocks new furniture recipes! [NPC] Carpenter: Some furniture is available exclusively in the Furniture Shop downstairs. Check it out!|
|[[Village]] After giving 64 [[Wool]]|[NPC] Carpenter: Check out the Furniture Shop downstairs!|
|[[Village]] After giving 64 [[Wool]]|[NPC] Carpenter: The Furniture Shop is downstairs. Purchase cool furniture down there!|
|[[The Garden]] First Order|[NPC] Carpenter: Hey, <playername>. I found this blueprint laying around in my house, just collecting dust. Thought it might catch your interest.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing the first order|[NPC] Carpenter: Here you go! Hopefully, you'll make better use of it than I ever could.|
|[[The Garden]] Subsequent orders before building the [[Greenhouse]]|[NPC] Carpenter: Hey, I noticed you haven't used that blueprint I gave to you. Did you lose it? [NPC] Carpenter: Luckily for you, I made a copy!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing the above order|[NPC] Carpenter: Here you go! Don't misplace it this time. It would've done just the same in my home.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying the above order|[NPC] Carpenter: No worries, I'll hang on to it for now. I'll come back later.|
|[[The Garden]] [[Greenhouse]], before speaking to [[Sam]]|[NPC] Carpenter: Ah, hello there! I have some information for you, but you need to finish talking to Sam first!|
|[[The Garden]] [[Greenhouse]], after speaking to [[Sam]]|[NPC] Carpenter: Looks like you could use some help with your Greenhouse. [NPC] Carpenter: Don't worry, I got you covered! [NPC] Carpenter: Talk to me any time to see your Greenhouse mutation progression.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Carpenter: Hey, how are you doing with the Greenhouse? That blueprint was complex.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Carpenter: Thank you! I'll be checking in to see how you're doing with the Greenhouse.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Carpenter: No worries, I'll come back later.|

# Carrot King Avatar
Categories: Non-player characters
[Carrot King Avatar]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[The Garden]]
The Carrot King Avatar is an [[NPC]] that only appears in [[The Garden]]. They can appear if the player consumes a [[Poignant Carrot Bait]]. Completing their offer rewards the player with a [[Poignant Lucky Clover]], along with the base rewards.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Carrot King Avatar: I crave vast heaps of carrots, my hunger ferrets!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Carrot King Avatar: I will hunger no longer.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Carrot King Avatar: How dare you let this hare hunger.|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|Added Carrot King Avatar.|
|Apr 1, 2026|Fixed Carrot King Avatar asking for thousands of Enchanted Golden Carrots ([[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]).|

|Apr 28, 2026|Added Carrot King Avatar.|

# Chantelle
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Chantelle]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Rewards the player with Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]]) and [[Carnival Tokens]] for playing games in the [[Carnival]].
Location: [[Carnival]]
Coordinates: -83.5 71 11.5
Chantelle is an [[NPC]] in the [[Carnival]] that gives the player various Carnival Goals, rewarding [[Carnival Ticket|Carnival Tickets]] and [[Carnival Tokens]] upon completion and also the player's stats in the various carnival games.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Chantelle: Ever feel like you're being watched? [NPC] Chantelle: Like your every action is being recorded and monitored? [NPC] Chantelle: Well, that sure is the case here at The Carnival... [NPC] Chantelle: ...but it's just so we can track your stats and let you complete challenges for rewards! [NPC] Chantelle: Don't think too hard about it.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Chantelle: I like to press a flower for each place the Carnival stops at. Do you have any?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Chantelle: Thank you! These smell amazing!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Chantelle: Oh, that's unfortunate. Maybe next time?|
## Trivia
- By completing all of Chantelle's goals, players can earn up to 1,200 [[Carnival Tokens]] and 75 (1 stack plus 11) [[Carnival Ticket|Carnival Tickets]] every time the Chivalrous Carnival event takes place.
- The amount of [[Carnival Tokens]] is increased by [[Party Equipment]].

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added Chantelle.|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Chantelle as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|

# Charlie
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Quests, Quest giver, Park
[Charlie]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Into the Woods ([[Quests]])
Description: Rewards the player with [[Charlie's Trousers]] and the [[Travel Scroll to The Park]] after providing 64 (1 stack) Birch Logs ([[Birch Log]]).
Location: [[Birch Park]]
Coordinates: -277.5 80 -17.5
Charlie is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Birch Park]]. They require the player to chop down 64 [[Birch Log|Birch Logs]] in exchange for [[Charlie's Trousers]] and the [[Travel Scroll to The Park]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Charlie: Howdy! Yer a friend of Lumber Jack, are ya? [NPC] Charlie: Well, works for me! [NPC] Charlie: I need 64 Birch Logs. Could you grab 'em for me? [NPC] Charlie: Do that, and then we can talk!|
|During Objective: Collect birch logs|[NPC] Charlie: I don't see 64 Birch Logs. Where are they?|
|Collecting 64 [[Birch Log]]|[NPC] Charlie: Cheers! Here, take these trousers for payment!|
|Next interaction|[NPC] Charlie: The Park is home to many different kinds of wood. Each kind has its own collection. [NPC] Charlie: Me and my mates each gather a specific type of wood, and then split it all evenly so we can all increase our different collections! [NPC] Charlie: I wanted Kelly to get some Spruce Logs for us today, but I've not seen her in a while... Can you maybe look for her in the Spruce Woods?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Charlie: What? Lumber Jack said I have a Treecapitator? In my dreams...|
|None|[NPC] Charlie: So much wood to chop, such little time...|
|None|[NPC] Charlie: You heard about that weird Cult over in the Dark Thicket? I’m considerin' joining them.|
## Trivia
- According to the [[Lumber Jack]], Charlie is his apprentice.

# Chef
Categories: Non-player characters
[Chef]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Hosts the Hot Dog Contest.
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -103 72.0625 -104
The Chef is an [[NPC]] located in the [[West Village]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. He hosts the Hot Dog Contest.
## Hot Dog Contest
The player can speak to the Chef to start a minute-long hot dog contest. During it, [[Hot Dog|Hot Dogs]] will be launched from a furnace, which the player must collect and bring to [[Joey McPizza]] by right-clicking the pressure plate in front of him. If 50 [[Hot Dog|Hot Dogs]] have been brought, the furnace is briefly replaced with an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Chef: About time you arrived, [player]! [NPC] Chef: Joey is a world-class competitive pizza eater and is trying to break into the hot dog scene. [NPC] Chef: Listen, it's simple. [NPC] Chef: 1. I cook the hot dogs. [NPC] Chef: 2. YOU put them on the plate in front of Joey. [NPC] Chef: 3. He eats. [NPC] Chef: Ready? Select an option: [START CONTEST]|
|Without having selected [START CONTEST]|[NPC] Chef: Hey [player], you're ready? Select an option: [START CONTEST]|
|Speaking to him during the contest|[NPC] Chef: Quick! [NPC] Chef: Collect the hot dogs!|
|Without having fed Joey ([[Joey McPizza]]) 50 Hot Dogs ([[Hot Dog]]) (without other players)|[NPC] Chef: Oops! [NPC] Chef: We didn't break the record of 50 hot dogs. [NPC] Chef: Mmh... maybe call a friend over and let's try again!|

# Chief Scorn
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Chief Scorn]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Barbarian Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Join the Barbarian Faction ([[Quests]]); Recover the Dean's Research ([[Quests]]); Recover Sulphur ([[Quests]])
Description: The leader of the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]).
Location: [[Chief's Hut]]
Coordinates: -580.5 115.5 -687.5
Chief Scorn is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Chief's Hut]] within [[Dragontail]], and is the leader of the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]]. His son is [[Ugo]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Whilst Chief Scorn Scolds [[Ugo]]|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo, where have you been? [NPC] Ugo: I- [NPC] Chief Scorn: You had ONE job! [NPC] Chief Scorn: 3 days later you come back with empty hands and a broken leg!? [NPC] Ugo: Well- [NPC] Chief Scorn: And you two, what are your names? [NPC] Elle: I'm- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Thank you for saving my rascal of a son. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Are you looking to join our ranks? [NPC] Elle: No but- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Perfect! [NPC] Chief Scorn: I welcome you both to the Barbarian Faction! [NPC] Chief Scorn: If you betray us you'll die. [NPC] Elle: I can't- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Enough talking! [NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo, go back out there and finish the job! [NPC] Ugo: But- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Guards, escort them out of my Hut!|
|After Stealing Research|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo why are you limping? [NPC] Ugo: My le- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Have you found the evil wizard machine? [NPC] Ugo: Y- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Give it to the guards, we will investigate it. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Enough with the mages and their shenanigans. [NPC] Ugo: Alri- [NPC] Chief Scorn: The Town Board is filling up and you are late on your Training Ugo. [NPC] Chief Scorn: What have you been doing? [NPC] Ugo: You- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Hurry up, failure isn't an option.|
|After completing [[Gris]]'s quest|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Newest member of the Faction! [NPC] Chief Scorn: The entire stash of Sulphur of the Piglin Faction has been stolen this morning. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I sent my son Ugo on a mission to recover the Sulphur. [NPC] Chief Scorn: He might be fighting the Mage Faction as we speak. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I implore you. Please assist Ugo in this operation. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I don't know what I would do without my son. My only son. [NPC] Chief Scorn: They have followed the traces of the Sulphur all the way to the Forgotten Skull in the North.|
|Before defeating [[Kuudra]]|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Please help Ugo.|
|After defeating [[Kuudra]]|[NPC] Chief Scorn: You finally retrieved the Sulphur. [NPC] Chief Scorn: What took you so long? [NPC] Ugo: A gian- [NPC] Chief Scorn: You even needed the help of those adventurers. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Do you have no shame? [NPC] Ugo: I didn't- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Look at the time. You are late to your training. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Guards, escort my son out of the hut. [NPC] Chief Scorn: <player> I am in your debt. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I cherish Ugo more than anything else in this world. [NPC] Chief Scorn: A father could not be prouder of his son. [NPC] Chief Scorn: My mind is at peace knowing you are by his side. [NPC] Chief Scorn: More importantly, I am glad he finally met someone he can consider his friend. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I taught him everything I could, but there are things only friends can teach. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I won't hold you any longer. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Good luck in your adventures.|
|Finished Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]])|[NPC] Chief Scorn: I have an important meeting to attend.|
|Finished Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]])|[NPC] Chief Scorn: I am very busy right now.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Greetings, I am in dire need of my favorite snack.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Chief Scorn: I lied about having an important meeting just to be here, glad it was worth it!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Chief Scorn: That is...not ideal!|
## Trivia
- Chief Scorn is the only NPC that speaks in bold text.
- It is possible to give Chief Scorn a [[Bachelor's Rose]] while in the [[Factions|ቾ Mage Faction]] if the player has met the 12,000 [[Factions|⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]] requirement. This can be done by teleporting near Chief Scorn and quickly giving the Bachelor's Rose to Chief Scorn before the guards teleport the player out.

# Coach Jackrabbit
Categories: Non-player characters
[Coach Jackrabbit]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Acts as an employee ([[Chocolate Factory]]) for the [[Chocolate Factory]].
Location: [[Rabbit House]]
Coordinates: 63.5 68 3.5
Coach Jackrabbit is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rabbit House]] that boosts the [[Chocolate]] generated in the [[Chocolate Factory]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: It's not about the individual leaps; it's about how we hop together as a team.|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: Visualize the win, then chase it down like it's the last carrot on Earth. You've got this!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: Sis' spirit is so fiery, I keep a bucket of water handy. Just in case her passion for change sets the place ablaze!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: Legacy isn't just passing on what you've accomplished. It's inspiring the next generation to hop even further. That's what we're doing here every day!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: I tell Hoppity - A dream without a plan is just a wish.|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: No bunny gets left behind in productivity! Remember, every hop is a step towards success!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: I tried showing Granny how to build muscle mass. [NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: I'm not welcome in the warren anymore.|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: Wake up! It's another glorious day to excel. If you're not moving, you're not improving. Now, hop to it!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: Rabbits, remember, the only time you should ever look back is to see how far you've hopped!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: When it comes to keeping calm, Daddy's cooler than a cucumber in a freezer. [NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: Sometimes I check if he's still breathing!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: Daddy's the kind of steady you need in a storm. If he was any more laid back, he'd be horizontal. But that's why we never tilt!|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: I start my mornings with a protein shake, then I have a mid-morning protein shake, followed by an after-lunch shake, and finish with a pre-bed shake. [NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: It's not just a routine; it's a ritual.|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: I'm so jacked it's unreal.|
|Employed|[NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: The Rabbits don't want me to enter their warren. They say I'm toxic or something. [NPC] Coach Jackrabbit: It's cool though, I know they are grinding away without me!|
## Trivia
- Coach Jackrabbit appears once the player is [[Chocolate Factory]] prestige IV.

# Cold Enjoyer
Categories: Non-player characters
[Cold Enjoyer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about the mechanics behind [[Cold]] and [[Cold Resistance]].
Location: [[Dwarven Base Camp]]
Coordinates: -15.25 136.781 217.25
Cold Enjoyer is an [[NPC]] within the [[Dwarven Base Camp]]. The player can talk to them to learn about [[Cold]] and [[Cold Resistance]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: I've figured out exactly how this Cold stuff works! Select an option: [How do I get Cold slower?] [How does Cold affect my Mining Speed?] [How does Cold affect my Walk Speed?]|
|Selecting [How do I get Cold slower?]|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: For every 1 Cold Resistance you have, Cold builds up 1% slower. [NPC] Cold Enjoyer: So, if you have 100 Cold Resistance, Cold will take 2x as long to build up! [NPC] Cold Enjoyer: You can get Cold Resistance from some Heart of the Mountain perks, and from certain items.|
|Selecting [How does it affect my Mining Speed?]|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: Your Mining Speed is reduced by 0.5% for every Cold you have. [NPC] Cold Enjoyer: The formula is: ( Current Cold - Cold Resistance ) / 2 = Mining Speed Penalty [NPC] Cold Enjoyer: So, if you have 70 Cold and 5 Cold Resistance, your Mining Speed will be reduced by 7.5%. Select an option: [What happens if I have more Cold Resistance than Cold?]|
|Selecting [What happens if I have more Cold Resistance than Cold?]|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: If your Cold Resistance is greater than the amount of Cold you have, you won't receive a Mining Speed reduction! [NPC] Cold Enjoyer: Since 99 Cold is the most you can have before dying, a Cold Resistance stat of 99 or more will be enough to ensure you never receive a Mining Speed Penalty.|
|Selecting [How does it affect my Walk Speed?]|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: Every 25 Cold, your speed cap will be reduced by 25%. [NPC] Cold Enjoyer: By the time you're at 75 Cold, you'll be moving at a snail's pace... [NPC] Cold Enjoyer: If you want to keep moving, keep warm!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: In order to better understand the cold, sometimes you have to get warm.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: We're chill, you and I.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Cold Enjoyer: I am not a [player] enjoyer.|
## Trivia
- This NPC is second in what can be assumed to be a series of NPCs that explain specific mechanic in detail, the first of them is [[Maths Enjoyer]].

# Cole
Aliases: Candidate Cole, Cole the Miner, Mayor Cole, Minister Cole
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Cole Cole]
Also known as: Mayor Cole
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Prospection Mining minions work 25% faster.; Mining XP Buff Get +60☯ Mining Wisdom on public islands.; Mining Fiesta Gain +25☯ Mining Wisdom, +25%  Mining Fortune, and collect Refined Minerals and Glossy Gemstones from mining!; Molten Forge Decrease the time it takes to forge items by 25%.; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Cole!
Cole the Miner is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. His perks focus around [[Mining]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Cole may run for election with up to 4 possible perks.
  [[Cole]] Cole the Miner
1. Prospection: [[Mining]] minions work 25% faster.
2. Mining XP Buff: Get +60 [[Mining Wisdom]] on public islands.
3. [[Mining Fiesta]]: Grants +25 [[Mining Wisdom]], +25% [[Mining Fortune]] and ability to drop [[Refined Mineral]] and [[Glossy Gemstone]] from mining. [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Packrat Shard]] can be bought from [[Brynmor]] while the perk is active.
4. Molten Forge: Decrease the time it takes to [[The Forge|forge]] items by 25%.
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Cole: Oi, mining is tough, but has t'be done! [NPC] Cole: I'm no different than yu' pal, I just work twice as hard!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Cole: Nothin's better than a full belly after a hard day of work!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Mayor Cole: Break is over, back to the mines I go!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Cole: Out of stock? sigh|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Cole: Some people mine to forget, I mine to remember.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Cole: A good pickaxe, is a swinging pickaxe!|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Cole: Nothin' is more satisfying, than a full inventory after a day of work!|
## Trivia
- Unlike [[Heart of the Mountain|Quick Forge]], Cole's Molten Forge perk only applies when the forging process is started and not retroactively.
- The line of dialogue: "Some mine to forget, I mine to remember" might be a reference to the song Hotel California, one of the lyrics being, some dance to remember, some dance to forget!

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Colore: Welcome to the Tourist Office! [NPC] Colore: This town was built four centuries ago, or more! [NPC] Colore: Visitors come from all around the world to visit our Cathedral. [NPC] Colore: But maybe you are more interested in our famous floating water fountain? [NPC] Colore: Or the Statue of the Hero? [NPC] Colore: There is so much to see!|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Colore: If you have any tourist-related questions, I'll be right here!|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Colore: Enjoy your time in Scarleton!|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Mage 1/4)|[NPC] Colore: Oh, a new face! Welcome to the City of Magic! [NPC] Colore: We used to be Pig Farmers centuries ago, but thanks to Sulphur we are now superior beings capable of mastering the magical elements! [NPC] Colore: If you are interested in tourist attractions I suggest you visit our Cathedral, which is also our Academy now.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); Less than 1,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Colore: [amount] Intelligence! That's...ok I guess!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); 1,000-2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Colore: [amount] Intelligence! Wow that's not bad at all!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); More than 2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Colore: [amount] Intelligence! What, how? Who are you even?|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 3/4)|[NPC] Colore: Have you tried the new ability phone? [NPC] Colore: The new one is barely better than the one from last year, people still buy them like sheep... [NPC] Colore: Wait, you don't even have an Abiphone? [NPC] Colore: Yikes...|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 4/4)|[NPC] Colore: Ah, the Piglins yes, quite troublesome they are. [NPC] Colore: I wouldn't go there if I were you.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Colore: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Colore: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Colore: Please stop bothering me.|

# Coral
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Coral]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Collects [[The Fish Family]].
Location: [[Red House]]
Coordinates: -626.5 99 -87.5
Coral is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Red House]]. They task the player with collecting varieties of [[The Fish Family]] in exchange for [[Fishing]] experience, [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], and [[Respiration]].
## Purpose
Coral rewards players with [[Fishing]] experience, [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], and [[Respiration]] for making their [[The Fish Family|Fish]] [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]].
The first 15 fish grant [[SkyBlock Levels|+3 SkyBlock XP]], for a total of up to [[SkyBlock Levels|+45 SkyBlock XP]]. Each fish also grants +2 [[Respiration]], as well as increasing amounts of [[Fishing]] XP.
|Amount|Fishing XP|
|-|-|
|1st-5th|+10,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|6th-10th|+15,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|11th-21st|+20,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|22nd-29th|+30,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|30th-34th|+40,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Coral: Have you ever heard tales of the Fish Family? [NPC] Coral: They're the curious group that trails behind Shrimp, you know? Select an option: [Vaguely.]|
|After selecting [Vaguely.]|[NPC] Coral: I see. Would you like to delve deeper into their story? Select an option: [Yes.]|
|After selecting [Yes.]|[NPC] Coral: Hmm. [NPC] Coral: Just a moment, let me gather my thoughts. [NPC] Coral: Are you truly eager to uncover more about them? Select an option: [Get on with it.]|
|After selecting [Get on with it.]|[NPC] Coral: Very well. [NPC] Coral: Bring members of the Fish Family to me and I will reward you greatly! [NPC] Coral: I just like to look at them, you see.|
|Donating a new fish for the first time|[NPC] Coral: You found a new Fish! [NPC] Coral: Great. Let's add it to the collection! The Fish Family is coming together swimmingly! [NPC] Coral: But hold on... for that I would need to make your Fish Co-op Soulbound. [NPC] Coral: Which means you will not be able to trade it anymore. [NPC] Coral: Are you alright with that? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|After selecting [NO]|[NPC] Coral: That’s too bad. Come back when you are sure. [NPC] Coral: That was a lovely fish...|
|After selecting [YES]|[NPC] Coral: Perfect, in that case I won't ask you that question again.|
|Donating a new fish after having donated one|[NPC] Coral: A new Fish! How delightful. [NPC] Coral: As a token of my appreciation, I've given you [rewards]!|
|Attempting to donate [[Oops the Fish]], [[Spook the Fish]], [[Chill the Fish]], or [[Egg the Fish]]|[NPC] Coral: Where is that one coming from? Not interested sorry!|
## Trivia
- Although [[Oops the Fish|Oops]], [[Spook the Fish|Spook]], [[Chill the Fish|Chill]], and [[Egg the Fish|Egg]] count as part of The Fish Family, Coral does not reward the player for showing those obtained from fishing in the Main Lobby of Hypixel.
- The [[Fishing]] XP gained from Coral is not affected by [[Fishing Wisdom]].

# Corm
Categories: Non-player characters
[Corm]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tells the story of the Great Dragons.
Location: [[The Bastion]]
Coordinates: -668.5 126 -924.5
Corm is an [[NPC]] near [[The Bastion]] that tells the story of the Great Dragons.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Corm: Are you a tourist? [NPC] Corm: This is my favorite site, I know everything about the great Dragons. [NPC] Corm: This is of course Jythar, the Eternal One. [NPC] Corm: The dragon army attacked our dimension many centuries ago and sent the world into pieces. [NPC] Corm: The skeleton of Chozon can be seen on the island south of here. He was one of the generals of Jythar. [NPC] Corm: Dogen, Kirva, and Daydro perished and fell in the void, to my knowledge. [NPC] Corm: The 4 generals were so huge that their reach could cover the sun and bring shadows upon entire cities. [NPC] Corm: Thousands of Dragons attacked that day and all were defeated, but did they fail? [NPC] Corm: What even was their purpose? [NPC] Corm: I think they decided to break the world to weaken us, and by us I mean everyone in this dimension. [NPC] Corm: Despite the wars raging at the time, we were still able to unite and beat this common enemy. [NPC] Corm: Jythar knew there would be no path to victory against us for as long as we could unite. [NPC] Corm: But why not retreat? [NPC] Corm: Why did he keep attacking us until his death? [NPC] Corm: I doubt it was madness. Dragons live for millennia, their Emperor surely wasn't mad. [NPC] Corm: So why would he give up his life to guarantee a potential victory in the future? [NPC] Corm: It's impossible for us to know, but we can theorize. [NPC] Corm: It is known from the Dragons summoned nowadays that they worship their Queen. [NPC] Corm: Queen, Empress - the exact word might have been lost in translation. [NPC] Corm: I think Jythar did it for her. [NPC] Corm: I think the reason is love. [NPC] Corm: Maybe I am wrong, but I choose to believe it. [NPC] Corm: This titan next to us is known by many names, some call them the Calamity. [NPC] Corm: They are the enemies, but if they are motivated by love, maybe we can communicate with them. [NPC] Corm: What do you think? Am I too romantic, or did my explanation make sense?|
## Trivia
- Based on Corm's dialogue, it seems that Dragons have a language.
- Corm's dialogue is the first mention of both [[Jythar]] and the [[Dragon Queen]], both of which had their lore expanded in later updates.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Corm.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Corm.|

# Cowboy Nick

[Cowboy Nick]

Cowboy Nick is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village Plaza]]. He gives out the [[Horsezooka]], which allows the player to obtain [[Half-Eaten Carrot|Half-Eaten Carrots]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Howdy [Player's Name]! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: I'm havin' to feed me bunnies ya see? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: I had me farrier dabble into ye old smithin' to concoct one Horsezooka. [NPC] Cowboy Nick: So listen, take me Horsezooka and have it eatin' 3,000 Hay Bales and come back hey?|
|Before breaking 3,000 Hay Bales with the [[Horsezooka]]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Use the Horsezooka on the Hay Bales and break 3,000 of 'em!|
|Once 3,000 Hay Bales have been broken|You collected all the required hay!|
|After breaking 3,000 Hay Bales with the [[Horsezooka]]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Nice job kiddo! Select an option: [Why are the horses exploding?]|
|Selecting [Why are the horses exploding?]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Oy there ain't no nothing to worry about hehe! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: It's special effects! Some pizzaz! Cinéma! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Anyway! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Ye cowboy cares about him bunnies, so let's get em' some carrots eh? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Here, take [rabbit's name] and go on harvestin' some 50 carrots in the Half-Eaten Cave.|
|Without 50 [[Half-Eaten Carrot]]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Stand behind [rabbit's name] to motivate it to hop forward! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Have ye bunny eat the carrot patches! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Bring back 50 carrots!|
|With 50 [[Half-Eaten Carrot]]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Sweet! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Uh wait... [NPC] Cowboy Nick: All these carrots are half-eaten! Select an option: [(rabbit's name) nibbed at them.]|
|Selecting [(Rabbit's Name) nibbled at them.]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Okay fair enough. [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Then... [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Do ya think you could get [rabbit's name] to nib a lot more and craft some 7 Nearly-Whole Carrots?|
|Without 7 [[Nearly-Whole Carrot]]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: The other rabbits don't want to eat no Half-eaten Carrots... [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Can ya bring me 7 Nearly-Whole Carrots? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: One for each bunny aroun'!|
|With 7 [[Nearly-Whole Carrot]]|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Fantastic! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Now we'll be havin' reserves for all o' winter! [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Thanks [player]!|
|After Completing Quest|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: What style of music do rabbits listen to? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Hip-Hop!|
|After Completing Quest|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: What do you call a very rich bunny? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: A billion-hare!|
|If the rabbit dies|SPLASH! Your rabbit [name] has been squashed!|
|If the rabbit isn't alive|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Here's [rabbit's name]!|
|If the rabbit isn't alive|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Oh ya lost [rabbit's name]? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: No biggie! It's right here|
|If the rabbit isn't alive|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Something happened to [rabbit's name]? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Well well, plenty where it came from!|
|If the rabbit isn't alive|[NPC] Cowboy Nick: Need ye favorite rabbit [rabbit's name]? [NPC] Cowboy Nick: Here it is!|

# Creed
Categories: Non-player characters
[Creed]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Complains that [[Barry]] won't give them 300 [[Motes]].
Location: [[Barry Center]]
Coordinates: -15.2 73 -112
Description: Works for [[Barry]] after they've been given 300 [[Motes]].
Location: [[Barry HQ]]
Coordinates: -51.5 54 -137.62
Creed is an [[NPC]] who first appears in the [[Barry Center]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They start out as a protestor against [[Barry]], but after the player handles their complaints, they move to [[Barry HQ]] and become a worker.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Creed: Barry is a terrible leader! [NPC] Creed: He doesn't want to hear us out!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Creed: Barry is hiding from us! [NPC] Creed: He doesn't want to face reality!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Creed: Someone promised donuts when coming to this protest. [NPC] Creed: And I don't see ANY DONUT! [NPC] Creed: Screw Barry!|
|After [[Barry]] says to see protestors|[NPC] Creed: Barry is the Prime Minister and he's still not giving me Motes? [NPC] Creed: Then why is he Prime Minister? [NPC] Creed: I demand at least 100 Motes! [NPC] Creed: Wait no... 300 Motes! I should tell Barry about Creed's complaints...|
|After telling Barry about Creed's complaints|[NPC] Creed: I demand at least 100 Motes! [NPC] Creed: Wait no... 300 Motes! Select the appropriate Barry rhetoric: ① [Give 300 Motes] ② [Barry says he can't afford 300 Motes, he's poor.] ③ [What did the duck say after he went shopping?]|
|Selecting ③ [What did the duck say after he went shopping?]|[NPC] Creed: What?? Select an option: [Put it on my bill.]|
|Selecting [Put it on my bill.]|[NPC] Creed: Yeah tell that to my kid who need braces.|
|Selecting ② [Barry says he can't afford 300 Motes, he's poor.]|[NPC] Creed: And? [NPC] Creed: I NEED those 300 Motes [NPC] Creed: Can't Barry just ask for Motes from someone, like everyone else is doing?|
|Selecting ① [Give 300 Motes] (without enough)|You cannot afford this!|
|Selecting ① [Give 300 Motes] (with enough)|[NPC] Creed: Finally. [NPC] Creed: I'll support Barry during his term, I guess.|
|In [[Barry HQ]]|[NPC] Creed: What am I going to spend my 300 Motes on? [NPC] Creed: Nothing... I just wanted to have more Motes.|
## Trivia
- While in [[Barry Center]], Creed and the other protestors have dialogue above their heads, such as "Barry bad!", "Go away Barry!", "Barry is trash.", "Go away Barry!", "Barry boooo!", and "Barry sucks!"

# Dante
Aliases: Candidate Dante, Mayor Dante
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Mayor, The Uprising, Events, Mayor Election
[Dante Dante]
Also known as: Mayor Dante
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Economy Restoration — Double AH and Bazaar taxes — 10% coin tax on shops — 5 coins per minute for chatting — 2 coin toll on travelling between islands; Increase Security — Hired goons to protect official buildings — Cancelled hazardous events — Decreased sunlight, prevent sunburn __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Dante!
Dante was a one time special Mayoral Candidate that appeared in the Year 128 [[Mayor Election]]. He offered three perks, Order, Security and Freedom, which changed once he was elected. After Dante was elected, [[Clerk Seraphine]] started building the [[Resistance Movement]] at the [[Dark Auction]], which lead to an election for the [[Resistance Leader]] to lead [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]], the final boss fight in which players kill Dante.
Dante's banner
## Mayoral Perks
Most special mayors run with up to four unique perks, but Dante has only been seen with three perks.
Perks:
1. Order: Establish order
2. Security: Strengthen security
3. Freedom: Freedom from fear
However, when Dante was elected, his real perks are revealed and are as follows:
Changes List:
- Economy Restoration (Establish Order)
  - Double [[Auction House|AH]] & [[Bazaar]] Tax
  - 10% [[Coins|coin]] tax on [[Shops]]
    - All NPC items have had their price increased by 10%. This did not affect selling items to NPCs.
  - 10 [[Coins]] per minute fee for chatting
  - 2 [[Coins]] toll on travelling between islands
    - This perk did not affect the /warpforge command.
- Increase Security (Strengthen Security)
  - Hired goons to protect [[Official Buildings]]
    - [[Goons]] would deny entry to most of the [[Official Buildings|public buildings]], excluding the [[Community Center]] where Dante is located, for players who were not wearing [[Happy Mask]] or did not have a [[Dante's Talisman]] with them.
  - Cancelled hazardous events
    - The [[Traveling Zoo]], [[Spooky Festival]] and [[Season of Jerry|Season of Jerry Event]] were canceled. The [[New Year Celebration]] was still held.
    - The [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] were replaced with [[Mayor Election|Dante Elections]], where only Dante could be elected.
  - Decreased sunlight, prevent sunburn
    - The time of the day on the entire server was set to midnight, no matter what the actual in-game time was. This perk was not added until several days after Dante was elected. (This didn't affect islands with a [[Day Saver]].)
- In the hours leading up to [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]], Dante introduced several more changes:
  - [[Dungeons]] were banned, which means no players could participate in Dungeons.
  - [[Pets]] were disabled, meaning no Pets could be equipped.
  - The alliance with the Dwarves was disbanded, barring access to the [[Dwarven Mines]].
  - The [[Auction House|AH]] and [[Bazaar]] were disabled, preventing people from buying items.
  - Eventually, traveling between islands itself was banned, forcing everyone into the [[Hub Island|Hub]] to fight Dante.
All of Dante's changes were reverted when [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] became mayor following [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Dante: Things have been getting out of control lately. [NPC] Dante: People are losing their values and other candidates are acting like everything is fine. [NPC] Dante: They simply don't have the grip required to lead this great land.|
|In [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Dante: Values. Progress. Justice [NPC] Dante: I will stabilize this rapidly declining system, permanently. [NPC] Dante: I will fix the declining economy, but above all things, safety is my priority. [NPC] Dante: Power needs to be in the right hands.|

[BOSS] Dante: Mining Islands have now been outlawed, effective immediately. We will be considering outlawing Mining in general too, but that's for another time. It's just too dangerous. [BOSS] Dante: Island Transportation has been outlawed. Nowhere is safer than home. [BOSS] Dante: I am hearing rumblings of a resistance led by Technoblade. Anyone found to be assisting this futile attempt will be CRUSHED. ## Message in the Hub, from Dante
After the popular Hypixel YouTuber Technoblade went live about the [[Resistance Movement]], a Twitter message from Hypixel's official Twitter, revealed a message from Dante. This message replaces the map in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
Click to see Dante's message
## Boss Fight
[Dante]
The Hub in ruins after the Dante boss fight
During the boss fight against the [[Resistance Movement|Resistance]], Dante unleashed [[Goons]] into the hub. Once they were defeated, he morphed into a giant Slime and started destroying the [[Hub Island|Hub]]. He could only be damaged using the [[Staff of the Rising Sun]].
Receiving damage from Dante would result in the following message:
Dante struck you for # damage!
During the boss fight players were able to look up to support [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]. Doing so resulted in the following message in the chat:
ENERGY BALL: You are sending <maximum mana> mana to Technoblade!
After Dante was defeated, the following message appeared in the chat:
-----------------------------------------------------
DANTE IS NO MORE!
IT TOOK AN ARMY OF TENS OF THOUSANDS!
BUT WE PREVAILED!
THE SUN RISES AGAIN!
TIME TO START REBUILDING OUR VILLAGE!
-----------------------------------------------------
## Dante Tomb
After 0.11.4, Dante's Tomb appeared in the [[Graveyard]] at [[Coordinates|-89, 75, -133]].
The tomb features mostly gray palate-type blocks of Stone and some Cobblestone Walls. There is a gray Dante statue standing on top of the tomb. Clicking the statue shows the following message in chat:
In Memoriam
While Dante will be remembered as a cruel tyrant, his righteous ideals live on in people's hearts.
dante best
## Trivia
- Jayavarmen mentioned a new special candidate in a tweet stating, "don't vote for them." This ended up to be Dante.
- Dante seems to be the opposite of [[Derpy]], as [[Derpy]] appears to want chaos while Dante wants order.
- Dante is the fourth special mayor, replacing the spot of [[Derpy]] in Year 128.
- When he got elected, a secret [[Resistance Movement|movement]] in the [[Dark Auction|Dark Auction House]] appeared.
- Before April 12, one wouldn't lose coins upon dying even if they didn't have a Cookie Buff or a Piggy Bank. This turned out to be a glitch and got patched.
- Dante, once he got elected:
  - Introduced a new soundtrack (made by ShinkoNet) into the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
  - Drained the color from buildings in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] and modified them. For example, the fish on the [[Fishing Merchant|Fish Merchant's]] house turned into a fish skeleton and the [[Event Stand]] was boarded up.
  - Changed the [[Hub Island|Hub]] biome to desert.
  - Replaced the well in the [[Village]] with his [[Golden Dante Statue|statue]].
  - Replaced normal [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] with [[Mayor Election|Dante Elections]] in which he's the only candidate.
  - Changed the result of the [[Mayor Election|election]] he's been elected in into 100% votes for him, in-game displayed as "100% - All Votes" instead of the true percentage and amount of votes.
  - Added a message from him against the resistance replacing the hub map, lasting for the time he is elected.
  - Hung green-gray-black banners on some buildings in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
- The message Dante had in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] had a chance to be glitched on some lobbies.
- After [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]] has ended, saying "dante" in the chat triggered the in-game swear filter. This was removed a few weeks later.
- Dante introduced 3 soundtracks, which are Dark and Seedy, Dark and Seedy (Pre-Revolt) and Breathless Encounter.
- When in boss form, even though Dante had a health bar, when he reached the wizard tower his health bar would always drop to 0%, no matter what.
- The [[Commands|command]] /killdante was used in Technoblade's Uprising livestream. The command is now disabled.
  - The command was later referenced with [[Bacte]] and [[Aura]].
- A memorial to Dante exists in the [[Graveyard]] at [[Coordinates|-90, 75, -130]]. You can find more information [[Graveyard|here]].
- Dante and [[Bacte]] share a striking resemblance, but aren't necessarily related.
- According to [[Elise (Rift)|Elise]], Wizards believe that [[Shen]] employed, or perhaps even created Dante. Another possibility is that [[Shen]] got in the [[Rift Dimension]] before the Wizards, and found Dante there.

David Hunterborough is an [[NPC]] located in [[Tangleburg]]. They introduce [[Hunting]], keep track of [[Hunting]] Milestones, and grant the player [[David's Cloak]].

David Hunterborough offers several Milestones for [[David's Cloak]]. These are for:
- Unlocking unique [[Attributes]]
- Syphoning [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]]
- Hunting Combat creatures
- Hunting Water creatures
- Hunting Forest creatures
- Obtaining [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] from [[Huntraps]]
- Obtaining [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] from [[Salts]]

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before unlocking [[Heart of the Forest]]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: <player>! Have you talked to Hina at the front of the island to get your hands on a Heart of the Forest?|
|Before completing [[Swoop]]'s tasks|[NPC] David Hunterborough: I talked to Swoop the other day, he explained a lot of interesting Foraging Mechanics to me. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Perhaps you should talk to him too, he’s at the entrance of the Village!|
|First Interaction|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Greetings! [NPC] David Hunterborough: I'm David Hunterborough, the island's Hunting Specialist. Select an option: [Hunting?]|
|Selecting [Hunting?]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Hunting has been around for eons, perfected by advanced beings known as the Precursors. [NPC] David Hunterborough: They discovered that within every creature lies a power unique to its kind. Select an option: [So, how do we access it?]|
|Selecting [So, how do we access it?]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Some creatures can be tamed, granting you their full power, as you may have seen with Pets. [NPC] David Hunterborough: But most creatures cannot be tamed, so hunters extract what we call a Shard. Select an option: [A Shard?]|
|Selecting [A Shard?]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Exactly. It represents the creature's biological footprint, its core attribute. [NPC] David Hunterborough: By using a Shard, you harness that unique power yourself. Select an option: [Interesting. What does a Shard look like?]|
|Selecting [Interesting. What does a Shard look like?]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: It's better if you start hunting to find out for yourself. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Talk to Alan and buy your first hunting weapon to get started.|
|Before buying a Huntaxe ([[Huntaxes]]) from [[Alan]]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: It's better if you start Hunting yourself! [NPC] David Hunterborough: But first, you should go talk to Alan and get proper gear.|
|After buying a Huntaxe ([[Huntaxes]]) from [[Alan]]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Perfect! You have your very first hunting weapon. [NPC] David Hunterborough: That's all you need to get started on your hunting journey. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Head over to Oden, they'll teach you the basics of hunting. [NPC] David Hunterborough: You'll find them north of the village!|
|Before completing [[Oden]]'s and [[Albert]]'s tasks|[NPC] David Hunterborough: First you should talk to Oden, they will show you the basics of Hunting. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Go find them North of the Village!|
|After buying a [[Small Pocket Black Hole]] from [[Albert]]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Wow! [NPC] David Hunterborough: It only took you ##:## to complete that objective! [NPC] David Hunterborough: [time-based message, see below] [NPC] David Hunterborough: You got your very first Combat Shard! [NPC] David Hunterborough: You can find all your Shards in your Hunting Box, inside your Hunting Skill Menu! [NPC] David Hunterborough: But there are various types of monsters out there you can Hunt. [NPC] David Hunterborough: You should go talk to Jaeger next! [NPC] David Hunterborough: You will find them close to the Loch.|
|Repeats until spoken to [[Jaeger]]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: But there are various types of monsters out there you can Hunt. [NPC] David Hunterborough: You should go talk to Jaeger next! [NPC] David Hunterborough: You will find them close to the Loch.|
|After completing [[Jaeger]]'s task|[NPC] David Hunterborough: You already completed your training with Jaeger? [NPC] David Hunterborough: It only took you ##:## to complete that objective! [NPC] David Hunterborough: [time-based message, see below] [NPC] David Hunterborough: You got your very first Water Shard! Select an option: [Does the type of the Shard matter?]|
|Selecting [Does the type of the Shard matter?]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: It does! [NPC] David Hunterborough: But that is still too early for you to learn. [NPC] David Hunterborough: You have to complete your training, with Auryon. [NPC] David Hunterborough: They should be South of the Village.|
|Before completing [[Auryon]]'s task|[NPC] David Hunterborough: You should go talk to Auryon next! [NPC] David Hunterborough: They should be South of the Village.|
|After completing [[Auryon]]'s task|[NPC] David Hunterborough: You managed to complete your Hunting training! [NPC] David Hunterborough: It only took you ##:## to complete that objective! [NPC] David Hunterborough [time-based message, see below] [NPC] David Hunterborough: You got your very first Forest Shard! [NPC] David Hunterborough: Combat, Water, and Forest are the only 3 types of Shards. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Dont ask me why, I have no idea! [NPC] David Hunterborough: Now you should finally learn what Shards are for. [NPC] David Hunterborough: You should go talk to my assistant. [NPC] David Hunterborough: No, not Pounce, my other assistant Kysha, right next to the Fusion Machine in the back.|
|Before completing [[Kysha]]'s task|[NPC] David Hunterborough: You should go talk to my assistant Kysha, right next to the Fusion Machine in the back.|
|After completing [[Kysha]]'s task|[NPC] David Hunterborough: You've mastered the fundamentals of hunting! [NPC] David Hunterborough: At first, the attributes may seem weak, but over time they will grow stronger. [NPC] David Hunterborough: The more you syphon an attribute, the more powerful it becomes. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Hunters continue to discover new Shards every year. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Actually, I've got another gift for you... [NPC] David Hunterborough: I guess I'm in a generous mood today.|
|After receiving [[David's Cloak]]|[NPC] David Hunterborough: I crafted this Cloak some time ago. It seems to grow stronger with every hunt. [NPC] David Hunterborough: Use it to take your hunting skills to the next level! [NPC] David Hunterborough: Talk to me if you want to learn more about hunting or view your progress.|
|Time-based objective messages|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Very impressive!|
|Time-based objective messages|[NPC] David Hunterborough: I've seen worse!|
|Time-based objective messages|[NPC] David Hunterborough: You tried your best I guess!|
|Clicking the [[Hunting Toolkit]] nearby|[NPC] David Hunterborough: Ah, the Hunting Toolkit! [NPC] David Hunterborough: An absolutely essential hunting item! It allows you to rapidly swap between your hunting tools! [NPC] David Hunterborough: You should buy one sometime!|
## Trivia
- David Hunterborough is likely a reference to the British broadcaster and biologist David Attenborough, best known for his nature documentaries.

# Defunct Wizardman
Categories: Non-player characters
[Defunct Wizardman]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Appears after completing the [[Wizardman]]'s quest in the [[Wizardman Bureau]].
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]

Defunct Wizardman is an [[NPC]] found at the top of [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
Defunct Wizardman serves as an ending to [[Wizardman]]'s quest, as they tell the player to close [[The Breach]] atop [[The Mountaintop]]. They only appear after completing [[Wizardman]]'s final quest cutscene, which is activated from wearing the [[Wizard Face]] and his armor.

|Future Interactions|[NPC] Defunct Wizardman: Seek out the breach and fulfill your purpose. [NPC] Defunct Wizardman: Good luck.|

# Derpy
Aliases: Candidate Derpy, Mayor Derpy
Categories: Non-player characters, Confirmations needed, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Derpy Derpy]
Also known as: Mayor Derpy
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears around here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor.
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ QUAD TAXES!!!; Pay 4x the normal amount of taxes!; TURBO MINIONS!!!; Minions have double the output!; DOUBLE MOBS HP!!!; ALL monsters have double health!; MOAR SKILLZ!!!; Gain +50% more skill experience!; __________________________; Click to vote for Derpy!
Derpy is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event and one of the five special candidates. His perks focus around causing chaos.
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Derpy is a special candidate, and will run for election according to the [[Mayor Election|Special Candidates Election Cycle]].
  [[Derpy]] Derpy
1. QUAD TAXES!!!: Pay 4x the normal amount of taxes!
2. TURBO MINIONS!!!: Minions have double the output!
3. DOUBLE MOBS HP!!!: ALL monsters have double health!
4. MOAR SKILLZ!!!: Gain +50% more skill experience!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Derpy: Do things always have to get better? Spice is all that matters. [NPC] Derpy: I'm giving you a one time opportunity, don't blow it.|
|Random|[NPC] Derpy: gg|
|Random|[NPC] Derpy: ◐_◑|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Derpy: Have you noticed how...never mind you wouldn't understand. [NPC] Derpy: Let's do a staring contest, I always win!|
|None|[NPC] Derpy: I mostly sleep, but when I don't sleep, I wake. [NPC] Derpy: Everyone is always a complete lack of surprise.|
## Gallery
- Derpy's original skin
## Bugs
- DOUBLE MOBS HP!!! does not affect the [[Magma Boss]] (but still affects [[Magma Glare|Magma Glares]] and [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]]).
- At the beginning of Derpy's mayor term, TURBO MINIONS!!! reduces the time used in offline calculations of [[Minions]] by 50%.
  - The double output is applied as expected to the shortened offline time.
  - This affects the duration of limited duration [[Minion Fuel|Minion Fuels]], making the fuel take longer to expire in real time. However the total in-game time where the fuel is active remains the same.
    - E.g. an [[Inferno Minion Fuel]], which lasts 24 hours, will take 48 real life hours to expire, but it will still make 24 hours worth of doubled drops.
  - This can happen with any minions. It is not limited to [[Inferno Minion]] with [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
  - The timers of [[Minion Upgrades|cooldown upgrades]], [[Beacon|Beacons]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], and [[Capsaicin Eyedrops]] are not affected.
  - This stops happening after 1 day, 8 hours, and 45 minutes of Derpy's mayor term.
## Trivia
- Derpy used to have a different skin.
- Derpy was supposed to be up for reelection in Year 128 but was replaced by [[Dante]].
  - Derpy may be the opposite of [[Dante]], as Derpy wants chaos, but [[Dante]] wants order.
- Derpy was supposed to be up for reelection in Year 464 but was replaced by [[Aura]].
  - One of Aura's ministers picked the TURBO MINIONS!!! perk this year. However, this was bugged to not apply to offline calculations.
- The MOAR SKILLZ!!! perk affects Catacombs and Gabagool Pet experience.
- The QUAD TAXES!!! perk does not affect the time and listing fees of the [[Auction House]], only the tax for items sold over 1 million coins.
- The QUAD TAXES!!! perk replaced an old perk called AH CLOSED!!! which as described, closed the Auction House instead.

Detective Amog is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Murder House]] and several other locations. They ask the player to solve a reverse murder through going to different areas for clues.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Murder House]] First Interaction|[NPC] Detective Amog: Greetings. [NPC] Detective Amog: It is a pleasure to make your acquaintance. [NPC] Detective Amog: Are you the culprit of this wrongdoing? Select an option: [The culprit?]|
|[[Murder House]] Selecting [The culprit?]|[NPC] Detective Amog: In fact, do not reveal the answer just yet. [NPC] Detective Amog: Allow me to introduce myself. I am Special Agent Thomas Amog. [NPC] Detective Amog: I am currently investigating a most heinous reverse murder. Select an option: [A reverse murder?]|
|[[Murder House]] Selecting [A reverse murder?]|[NPC] Detective Amog: Yes. [NPC] Detective Amog: Observe the bloodstains on the ground, my dear. [NPC] Detective Amog: It is clear that a murder is about to occur. [NPC] Detective Amog: Someone is going to die here, it is inevitable. [NPC] Detective Amog: Might you assist me in solving this case? Select an option: [Sure.]|
|[[Murder House]] Selecting [Sure.]|[NPC] Detective Amog: Perfect, here is a Detective Scanner, take it.|
|[[Murder House]] After claiming the [[Detective Scanner]]|[NPC] Detective Amog: I suggest utilizing it on various objects within this abode to uncover clues as to the whereabouts of the hapless victim.|
|[[Murder House]] Without a [[Detective Scanner]]|[NPC] Detective Amog: Here is the scanner.|
|[[Murder House]] Without having scanned everything in the house|[NPC] Detective Amog: Are you sure you used the scanner on everything in this house?|
|[[Murder House]] Without having scanned everything in the house|[NPC] Detective Amog: This part is crucial for the investigation.|
|[[Murder House]] Without having scanned everything in the house|[NPC] Detective Amog: Use the Detective Scanner to uncover clues as to the whereabouts of the hapless victim.|
|[[Murder House]] After scanning everything in the house|[NPC] Detective Amog: I believe we have gathered all the necessary clues to unravel this mystery. [NPC] Detective Amog: Well done my dear! [NPC] Detective Amog: However, the question remains, where ought we to begin our investigation? Select an option: [Perhaps their lover is the florist?]|
|[[Murder House]] Selecting [Perhaps their lover is the florist?]|[NPC] Detective Amog: The bouquet was crafted with love, that much is certain. [NPC] Detective Amog: Well done, my dear, for deducing as much. [NPC] Detective Amog: We must make haste and locate the florist at once. Time is of the essence in this matter.|
|[[Murder House]] Without having spoken to Detective Amog or [[Marcia]] at the Village Plaza yet|[NPC] Detective Amog: We must make haste and locate the florist at once. Time is of the essence in this matter.|
|[[Village Plaza]] If you haven't finished speaking to [[Marcia]]|[NPC] Detective Amog: Marcia, she must be the lover.|
|[[Village Plaza]] After finishing speaking to Marcia; Without having spoken to Detective Amog or Master Tactician Fink at Around Colosseum yet|[NPC] Detective Amog: Ah, it appears Master Tactician Fink is the culprit. [NPC] Detective Amog: Most intriguing. [NPC] Detective Amog: Come, let us make haste to the Colosseum and apprehend him.|
|[[Around Colosseum]] Without having finished speaking to [[Master Tactician Fink]]|[NPC] Detective Amog: The culprit is right there, let's interrogate him.|
|[[Around Colosseum]] After obtaining the [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]]|[NPC] Detective Amog: Marvelous! We have discovered the instrument of the crime. [NPC] Detective Amog: However, the location of Ramero remains unknown. Select an option: [This is complicated.]|
|[[Around Colosseum]] Selecting [This is complicated.]|[NPC] Detective Amog: Yes, have you managed to uncover any promising leads? [NPC] Detective Amog: Think, my dear [player], think. Select an option: [What about that ring we found?]|

## Gallery
- Detective Amog briefly holding a Wallet at the top of The Mountaintop. Detective Amog briefly holding a [[Wallet]] at the top of [[The Mountaintop]].
## Trivia
- The name Detective Amog is likely a reference to the murder mystery game "Among Us".
- Visually, he is based on the character Rorschach from DC Comics.
- The quest for the [[Ring of Broken Love]] is in reference to the [[Ring of Love]] from the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest.

# Diaz
Aliases: Candidate Diaz, Diaz the Economist, Mayor Diaz, Minister Diaz
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Diaz Diaz]
Also known as: Mayor Diaz
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Long Term Investment The elected minister will appear as a candidate with all of their perks in the next election cycle.; Volume Trading The available item quantity per Shen's Auction has been doubled, and two additional Shen's Special auctions will be available if Diaz is Mayor.; Stock Exchange Participate in the Stonks Auction for a chance to win Stock of Stonks! Trade them for extravagant items at the Trade Center.; Shopping Spree Increase daily NPC buy limits by 10x.; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Diaz!
Diaz the Economist is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. Her perks focus around [[Coins]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Diaz may run for election with up to 4 possible perks.
  [[Diaz]] Diaz the Economist
1. Long Term Investment: The elected [[Mayor Election|Minister]] will appear in the next [[Mayor Election|Election]] with all of their available perks.
2. Volume Trading: Double the item quantity from [[Shen's Auction]], Shen's Special, and Rift Shen's on the year Diaz is elected. Two additional Shen's Special auctions will be available for the duration of Diaz being elected as Mayor.
3. [[Richard|Stock Exchange]]: Participate in the Stonks Auction for a chance to win [[Stock of Stonks]]! Trade them for extravagant items at the [[Trade Center]].
4. Shopping Spree: Increase daily [[NPC]] buy limits by 10x.
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diaz: You know what's good for you? More coins. [NPC] Diaz: I'm not talking small coins, I mean mountains of coins. [NPC] Diaz: You look smart, you and I can fix this economy.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Diaz: Made a bil profit this morning through some Bazaar flipping, always makes me real hungry.|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting|[NPC] Mayor Diaz: Tudelu, the profits never sleep.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Diaz: I'll undercut your orders, just watch.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diaz: I love the sound of coins in the morning.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diaz: My father invented piggy banks.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Diaz: One day I'll build the Diaz tower.|
## Trivia
- The Long Term Investment perk also applies to Diaz herself if she's elected minister and has the minister perk set to this perk.
- Due to both versions of [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Auctions]] refreshing on 1st of Late Spring and the new mayor taking office on 27th of Late Spring, the Volume Trading perk only affects the next auction.
- Diaz's Shopping Spree perk does not apply to [[Kiara]]'s shop.
- The Shopping Spree perk can be used to skip out on grinding certain items or to resell to the [[Bazaar (NPC)|Bazaar]] at a higher volume.
  - Due to how long they take to grind out, [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players often buy [[Flowers|Dandelions]] for [[Flower Minion]] upgrades, as well as [[Sponge]] and [[Wet Sponge]] for [[Sponge Armor]], while this perk is active.
- Diaz's old perk, Barrier Street, is most likely a reference to Wall Street.
- Before her perks were overhauled, Diaz was by far the least elected mayor.
- Prior to 0.20.3, Diaz was the only normal mayor to have 2 perks.
- Diaz holds the record for most time between elections, taking 98 years.
- Special Mayors cannot be Ministers. If runner-up is a Special Mayor, [[Nancy|Mayor Nancy]] will appear in a place of a Minister instead. This means that Long Term Investment perk doesn't work on Special Mayors.

|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Dirt Guy: Me like dirt.|
|When holding an item reforged with Dirty ([[Dirt Bottle]])|[NPC] Dirt Guy: Dirty!|
|Giving [[Dirt]]|[NPC] Dirt Guy: Dirt!|
|Giving [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]]|[NPC] Dirt Guy: DIRT! [NPC] Dirt Guy: PRECIOUS! You were given a Precious in exchange for your InfiniDirt™ Wand!|
|Interacting with an [[Unripe Jerryseed]] reforged with Dirty ([[Reforging]])|[NPC] Dirt Guy: Ooooh... this seed... needs dirt. [NPC] Dirt Guy: YES! Needs the dirty, need to be DIRTY! [NPC] Dirt Guy: applies the fertilizer [NPC] Dirt Guy: Happy growl Now... MUCH better. [NPC] Dirt Guy: Fertilized... precious!|
## Trivia
- Dirt Guy could be a reference to Sandman from several Spider-Man films.
- Dirt Guy's line of dialogue 'PRECIOUS!' is a reference to Gollum from J.R.R. Tolkien's The Lord of the Rings.
- Upon interaction, a dirt placing sound will play.
- There is a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] inside the cave he is at, which is located at [[Coordinates|22, 127, 184]].
- When given an [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]] whilst the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is in the [[Village]], he will give the player a [[Precious]] and take their [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]].

# Don Expresso
Aliases: Don Espresso
Categories: Non-player characters, Confirmations needed, Dwarven Mines
[Don Expresso]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Appears during the [[Mithril Gourmand]] event, demanding 200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Tasty Mithril]].
Location: [[Dwarven Village]]
Coordinates: 37 202 -123
Don Expresso appears during the [[Dwarven Mines|Mithril Gourmand]] event, located at [[Coordinates|37, 202, -123]] in the [[Dwarven Village]]. He asks the players to bring him 200 [[Tasty Mithril]], obtained by mining in a specified location. If he isn't fed in time, he falls to the ground and eventually disappears. If he is fed, he lies down and goes to sleep[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
One minute before Don Expresso disappears, the following chat message appears: Don Expresso is leaving in 1 minute! Make sure to feed him everything you have before he leaves!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Not giving [[Tasty Mithril]]|[NPC] Don Expresso: THIS ISN'T WHAT I ASKED FOR! BRING TASTY MITHRIL TO ME NOW!|
|Not giving [[Tasty Mithril]]|[NPC] Don Expresso: DISGUSTING! I'M GONNA GET YOU FIRED FOR FEEDING ME THIS GARBAGE!|
|Not giving [[Tasty Mithril]]|[NPC] Don Expresso: EWWW WHAT IS THIS?|
|When event starts|[NPC] Don Expresso: I HUNGER [NPC] Don Expresso: BRING ME EDIBLE MITHRIL|
|Bringing [[Tasty Mithril]] from a previous event|[NPC] Don Expresso: THIS OLD MITHRIL IS STALE! DON'T EVER FEED THIS TO ME AGAIN! # Tasty Mithril pieces were removed from a different event!|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I FORGOT TO BRING A SNACK|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: 0/10, WOULD NOT RECOMMEND, TAKING TOO LONG|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: WHAT ARE YOU GUYS WAITING FOR|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I AM LEAVING A BAD RATING FOR THIS PLACE ON MINEADVISOR|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I WANT TO SPEAK TO YOUR MANAGER|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: THEY LOOK SO GOOD|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I MIGHT JUST STAY HERE FOREVER THEN|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: DO YOU EVEN KNOW WHO I AM|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: MY FATHER WILL HEAR ABOUT THIS|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: WHY IS THIS TAKING SO LONG|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I AM RATING THIS ESTABLISHMENT ONE STAR ON DWELP|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I DON'T HAVE ALL DAY, I ONLY WANT THE BEST LOOKING MITHRIL PIECES!|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: MY MEALS SHOULD HAVE ALREADY BEEN READY|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I AM NOT HAPPY WITH HOW LONG THIS IS TAKING|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: I AM NOT OKAY WITH THIS|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: YOU SHOULD ALL BE ASHAMED OF YOURSELVES|
|Randomly during Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]])|[NPC] Don Expresso: FEED ME|
|After Mithril Gourmand ([[Dwarven Mines]]) ends|[NPC] Don Expresso: I THINK I MIGHT HAVE ROCK POISONING, I NEED TO LAY DOWN! [NPC] Don Expresso: I DON'T FEEL SO GOOD...|
## Tips
- The /tptodonexpresso command can be used to teleport to Don Expresso.
  - This command is executed by clicking on a chat message when Don Expresso is about to leave in 20 seconds, but it also works sooner than that.
## Trivia
- For a few seconds after Don Expresso disappears, he can still be interacted with, and his name tag will still be visible.
- Don Expresso's name is likely a reference to a Hypixel staff member, 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] Donpireso.
- Feeding Don Expresso [[Tasty Mithril]] for the first time will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"A Good Review" Achievement]].
- In his dialogue, "Mineadvisor" and "Dwelp" are references to the review apps TripAdvisor and Yelp.
- One of his dialogue lines MY FATHER WILL HEAR ABOUT THIS is likely a reference to threats that Draco Malfoy, a character, often made to Harry Potter and his friends in J. K. Rowling's book series Harry Potter.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Don Expresso.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Don Expresso.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Speaking to Don Expresso will now consume all of the player's [[Tasty Mithril]].|
