# Dr. Stone
Aliases: Dr Stone, Fossil Donations
Categories: Non-player characters, Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs
[Dr. Stone]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to Chisels ([[Chisel]]) and [[Fossils]]. Also grants one-time rewards after showing new fossils.
Location: [[Fossil Research Center]]
Coordinates: 28 120 238.5
Dr. Stone is an [[NPC]] located on in the [[Fossil Research Center]] in the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] who collects [[Fossils]] and unlocks several pets in [[The Forge]].
## Usage
Dr. Stone teaches the player about [[Chisel|Chisels]] and [[Fossils]]. Donating unique [[Fossils]] to them grants 25,000 [[Mining]] XP, 25,000 ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]], 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Fossil Essence]], 5,000 [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM EXP]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], and [[SkyBlock Levels|10 SkyBlock XP]], additionally unlocking a recipe in the [[The Forge|Forge]]. Donating all 8 [[Fossils]] to them grants an additional 50,000 ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] and 10,000 [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM EXP]][Confirm].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Hello! I am Dr. Stone, a prominent Paleontologist from lands afar. [NPC] Dr Stone: I've been able to set myself up down here for a short while in search of rare Fossils. I know the risks of doing so, but I just love research! [NPC] Dr Stone: I'm always on the lookout for Suspicious Scraps when I'm mining around in Glacite Mineshafts, which I sometimes find when in the Glacite Tunnels. [NPC] Dr Stone: If you ever find a scrap when mining in a mineshaft, simply put it in the Fossil Excavator over there! [NPC] Dr Stone: Sometimes you'll find little treasures, and sometimes you'll find an entire fossil! If you do find a fossil, show it to me! [NPC] Dr Stone: If you donate a fossil to me, I'll show you how to turn it into a pet! That way, if you ever find another, you can keep it! [NPC] Dr Stone: Here, take this Chisel. Now you just need a Suspicious Scrap! [NPC] Dr Stone: Looks like that odd fellow over by the campfire might know a thing or two about getting them...|
|Donating a Fossil ([[Fossils]])|[NPC] Dr. Stone: I've been after that one for ages.|
|Donating a Fossil ([[Fossils]])|[NPC] Dr. Stone: I've never seen one of these before!|
|Donating a Fossil ([[Fossils]])|[NPC] Dr. Stone: This one is a rare find!|
|Donating a Fossil ([[Fossils]])|[NPC] Dr. Stone: What a beauty!|
|Donating a Fossil ([[Fossils]])|[NPC] Dr. Stone: What a remarkable discovery!|
|Donating a Fossil ([[Fossils]])|[NPC] Dr. Stone: I can't believe you found this!|
|Interacting with an empty display case|[NPC] Dr. Stone: This display case is empty. If you bring me a fossil, I'd be happy to display it and tell you about it.|
|Interacting with a [[Helix Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: It's so small, but it's a Helix Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: That thing is 100% Ammonite! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Don't let their cute looks fool you, those things were predators! [NPC] Dr. Stone: If you're a slow-moving animal on the sea floor, these guys would have been your enemies.|
|Interacting with a [[Helix Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Ooh, you like the look of the Helix Fossil? [NPC] Dr. Stone: That's a very fossilized Ammonite, still completely intact! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Once upon a time, there were thousands of different species of Ammonite, and they were plentiful in numbers. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Today... not so much.|
|Interacting with a [[Helix Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Something just feels special about the Helix Fossil. [NPC] Dr. Stone: It's actually a well-preserved Ammonite from long ago. [NPC] Dr. Stone: While this one is pretty small, the largest known fossils of this variety are massive!|
|Interacting with an [[Ugly Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That thing looks gross. It's surely an Ugly Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Whatever Goblin that belonged to was pretty nasty-looking, even for a Goblin. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Goblins don't really think for themselves most of the time, and prefer to blindly follow the herd. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Kind of like the people over in the Hub when it comes to voting for a Mayor...|
|Interacting with an [[Ugly Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Ew! That's definitely an Ugly Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Looks like it belonged to an especially ugly Goblin. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Goblins are plentiful in other areas of the mines, but I'm yet to find any down here. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Maybe it got lost many years ago and ended up down here?|
|Interacting with an [[Ugly Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That in front of you is an Ugly Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Yeah, that name was the best we could come up with... [NPC] Dr. Stone: It's a skull that once belonged to a particularly deformed-looking Goblin. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Just looking at it is giving me the heebie-jeebies, let's move on!|
|Interacting with a [[Claw Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That Claw Fossil looks pretty daunting... [NPC] Dr. Stone: That was once attached to a Mole. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Those guys are solitary creatures and quite territorial, so you don't normally see them about.|
|Interacting with a [[Claw Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That Claw Fossil looks real menacing with those sharp claws... [NPC] Dr. Stone: But it becomes a bit less scary when you realize it came from a Mole. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Those little guys actually only live for a few years, which is kinda sad...|
|Interacting with a [[Claw Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Pretty sharp, huh? That's the Claw Fossil for you. [NPC] Dr. Stone: That was once attached to a Mole. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Those things are actually insectivores and love digging tunnels. [NPC] Dr. Stone: When they dig, they move their arms in an outward motion, like a human swimming in breaststroke!|
|Interacting with a [[Tusk Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That Tusk Fossil looks absolutely amazing. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Mammoths had two of these, and used them to fend off predators. [NPC] Dr. Stone: They also used them to fight each other over territory and gather food!|
|Interacting with a [[Tusk Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That thing there is a Tusk Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: That thing was once attached to a Mammoth , believe or not. [NPC] Dr. Stone: While people often think Mammoths were huge, they were actually around the size of your average modern-day elephant. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Though it's not like that fact makes their tusks any less intimidating...|
|Interacting with a [[Tusk Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Look how awesome! The Tusk Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: I wish I could see the Mammoth it belonged to. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Though they look pretty scary, these guys mostly ate vegetation. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Still, if you ever saw one you'd probably want to keep out of its way...|
|Interacting with a [[Webbed Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That in front of you is a Webbed Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: It belonged to a particularly resilient Penguin. [NPC] Dr. Stone: While on land, a group of penguins is called a waddle. [NPC] Dr. Stone: But, if they're in the water, they're instead referred to by researchers as a raft! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Which one do you like more?|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Aw! That little fella is a Webbed Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: It's all that remains of whatever little Penguin once lived around here. [NPC] Dr. Stone: The black-and-white look of most penguins is actually a form of camouflage. [NPC] Dr. Stone: When they're swimming, predators looking from above see the black on their backs, which camouflages with the water. [NPC] Dr. Stone: And, to predators below, their white underbelly blends in with the light shining down on the water from above!|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Ooh! One of my favorites! The Webbed Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Believe it or not, that once belonged to a Penguin! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Most bird species evolve with hollow bones, to help them stay light when flying. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Penguins, however, have solid bones, which help them float when in the water.|
|Interacting with a [[Clubbed Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: The Clubbed Fossil is quite majestic, I've never seen another tail like it. [NPC] Dr. Stone: It belonged to an Ankylosaurus. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Though this dino was covered in strong plating that would usually make it impervious to predators, it had a soft underbelly. [NPC] Dr. Stone: So, it was an easy meal... if you could roll it over first, and that thing weighed more than a dwarven forge!|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Dr. Stone: This one here looks a little strange, but is the Clubbed Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: It was once a tail belonging to an Ankylosaurus. [NPC] Dr. Stone: The Ankylosaurus was a herbivore, but it lived at the same time as the T-Rex. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Though, if you have to live at the same time as a bloodthirsty predator like the T-Rex, it probably would help to have an armored body and bone-shatteringly strong tail.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Ah, the Clubbed Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: That thing was once attached to the rear of an Ankylosaurus. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Those things were pretty much walking fortresses - completely covered in bony deposits known as osteoderms that protected them from predators. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Today, you'll see similar armor-like osteoderms on Crocodiles and Armadillos.|
|Interacting with a [[Footprint Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That there is a Footprint Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Once upon a time, it belonged to a ferocious T-Rex! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Those boys were bad news - they would take a bite out of anything! [NPC] Dr. Stone: There's even evidence to suggest that they would sometimes eat each other!|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Believe it or not, but that's a Footprint Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: It looks to have belonged to the legendary T-Rex! [NPC] Dr. Stone: The T-Rex is made fun of for its comparatively small arms, but they were still over three feet long! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Some other carnivores also had even stubbier little arms, so I guess there's that...|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That looks like a Footprint Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: That bad boy once belonged to the T-Rex! [NPC] Dr. Stone: The T-Rex actually had a brain twice as large as most other carnivores. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Some researchers believe it was smart enough to set traps for its prey, and actually functioned as an ambush predator.|
|Interacting with a [[Spine Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: A Spine Fossil! This one's pretty rare. [NPC] Dr. Stone: It was once part of a particularly interesting creature called the Spinosaurus. [NPC] Dr. Stone: This dinosaur is actually really controversial, as fossils make it hard to tell how it truly behaved. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Did it spend most of its time hunting on land, or was it more of an aquatic forager? [NPC] Dr. Stone: Either way, it's pretty funny. With every new discovery, the Spinosaurus loses a bit more of its mystique. [NPC] Dr. Stone: I can't believe Paleontologists are nerfing dinosaurs now.|
|Interacting with a [[Spine Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: The Spine Fossil! Now, this one is really cool! [NPC] Dr. Stone: This was once attached to the back of the Spinosaurus. [NPC] Dr. Stone: The Spinosaurus was not only a carnivore, but also one of the few dinosaurs to be an adept swimmer. [NPC] Dr. Stone: What I'm saying is that, no matter where you ran, it would probably follow. Nice.|
|Interacting with a [[Spine Fossil]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: That in front of you is a Spine Fossil! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Many, many, many years ago, it would have belonged to one Spinosaurus. [NPC] Dr. Stone: Fun fact, the T-Rex isn't actually the heaviest carnivorous dinosaur known to researchers! [NPC] Dr. Stone: That title actually goes to this fella, who was one BIG BOY!|
|After donating every type of [[Fossils]]|[NPC] Dr. Stone: Wow! Looks like you've found every type of fossil around! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Maybe we'll find more together in the future, but for now my work here is done! [NPC] Dr. Stone: Please, take these rewards. It's the least I can do! [NPC] Dr. Stone: I think I'll stick around for a little longer, and then it's onto my next adventure!|
## Trivia
- Dr. Stone's name might be a reference to an anime with the same name.

# Dulin
Categories: Non-player characters
[Dulin]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Beyond the Wall ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Allows the player to access the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]].
Location: [[Hanging Court]]
Coordinates: 80.25 187.3 127.5
Description: Talks about the [[Glacite Tunnels]].
Location: [[Dwarven Base Camp]]
Coordinates: 15.5 128 206.5
Dulin is an [[NPC]] found initially in the [[Hanging Court]], and later in the [[Dwarven Base Camp]]. He unlocks the [[Secret Railroad Pass]] [[The Forge|Forge]] recipe and grants access to the [[Glacite Tunnels]], and only appears upon reaching [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Initial Interaction|[NPC] Dulin: This is all that remains... [NPC] Dulin: Hello, {player}. Thank you for coming. [NPC] Dulin: I have heard of your triumphs in the Crystal Hollows, and thought we should meet. [NPC] Dulin: I am Dulin, descendant of Divan and rightful King of the Dwarven Mines. Those imbeciles in the palace took up their positions in my absence. [NPC] Dulin: I am preparing an expedition into the area beyond the Great Ice Wall, and need some adventurers to scout out the area for me. [NPC] Dulin: If I could sneak you in, would you be willing to help? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Selecting [YES]|[NPC] Dulin: Thank you. My people will remember your name for eons. [NPC] Dulin: I am glad that I have your trust, but I will need you to prove yourself before entering the Glacite Tunnels. [NPC] Dulin: The Ticket Master will only let you in if you have a Secret Railroad Pass to show him. [NPC] Dulin: You can put one together in the Dwarven Forge, if you can get the right materials.|
|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Dulin: Very well. But you and I, our fates are entwined. You'll come back, and I'll welcome you with open arms when you do.|
|Interacting With [[Secret Railroad Pass]]|[NPC] Dulin: A Secret Railroad Pass! Very well. [NPC] Dulin: Go show that to the Ticket Master, and he'll let you travel to the Glacite Tunnels. [NPC] Dulin: I'll see you down there!|
|Reaching the [[Dwarven Base Camp]]|[NPC] Dulin: Nice to see you here, {player}. [NPC] Dulin: Feel free to go on ahead, but don't stray too far into the Glacite Tunnels. [NPC] Dulin: From what I've heard, they go very deep down. The legends speak of some dwarves finding Mineshafts when mining! [NPC] Dulin: Anyways, if you need me I'll be here, waiting for my Expedition Team to arrive. [NPC] Dulin: They should be here within 3-5 business days, then we can set out!|
|Subsequent Interactions|[NPC] Dulin: Hey {player}! Have you found time to explore the Glacite Tunnels yet? [NPC] Dulin: The team should arrive shortly! They should be here within 3-5 business days, then we can set out!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Dulin: I suppose this is your very own kingdom in a sense, [player]? Splendid.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Dulin: Potatoes are a dwarf's favorite food. They are like us in many ways, springing to life from the soil under even the harshest conditions. [NPC] Dulin: That resolve is what makes us who we are and keeps our flame alight. [NPC] Dulin: We will push beyond the tunnels and retake what is ours, no matter the cost.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Dulin: This doesn't change things between us - you are still needed as a member of the Expedition Team.|
## Trivia
- Dulin states that he is a descendant of [[Divan (disambiguation)|Divan]], who is a currently unseen [[NPC]] to whom the discovery of the [[Divan Fragment]] and [[Divan's Alloy]] is attributed.
  - Dulin also states that he is the rightful king, meaning [[Divan (disambiguation)|Divan]] used to be king.
- Dulin could be based on the character of Durin from the Lord of the Rings.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 21, 2024|Added Dulin.|
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Dulin as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Added Dulin.|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Dulin as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|

# Dungeon Hub Selector

[Dungeon Hub Selector]

Location: [[Dungeon Hub]]

The Dungeon Hub Selector is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dungeon Hub]] that serves as a means to change [[Dungeon Hub]] server.
## Trivia
- The Dungeon Hub Selector does not offer the ability to warp to a small server, like the [[Hub Selector]] in the [[Village]].
  - Instead, the Dungeon Hub Selector offers to warp to back the [[Hub Island]].

# Dust
Categories: Non-player characters
[Dust]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Complains that [[Barry]] won't fix his own gambling mistakes.
Location: [[Barry Center]]
Coordinates: -12 71 -100.21
Description: Works for [[Barry]] after hearing about patience.
Location: [[Barry HQ]]
Coordinates: -54.406 54 -138.4
Dust is an [[NPC]] who first appears in the [[Barry Center]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They start out as a protestor against [[Barry]], but after the player handles their complaints, they move to [[Barry HQ]] and become a worker.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Dust: Barry is a terrible leader! [NPC] Dust: He doesn't want to hear us out!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Dust: Barry is hiding from us! [NPC] Dust: He doesn't want to face reality!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Dust: Someone promised donuts when coming to this protest. [NPC] Dust: And I don't see ANY DONUT! [NPC] Dust: Screw Barry!|
|After first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Dust: I gambled all of my coins away. [NPC] Dust: I lost my family. [NPC] Dust: I lost my friends. [NPC] Dust: And I lost my job. [NPC] Dust: How is Barry going to fix that? Uh? I should tell Barry about Dust's complaints...|
|After telling Barry about Dust's complaints|[NPC] Dust: Why would Barry be the Prime Minister if he can't even fix my own mistakes?! [NPC] Dust: Boooooooooooooooooooooooooooooo! Select the appropriate Barry rhetoric: ① [Something smart.] ② [Patience comes to those who wait.] ③ [The secret to life is good dental hygiene.]|
|Selecting ① [Something smart.]|[NPC] Dust: Something smart? [NPC] Dust: What??|
|Selecting ③ [The secret to life is good dental hygiene.]|[NPC] Dust: This could quite possible be the dumbest thing I've heard in my entire life. [NPC] Dust: Please do not approach me.|
|Selecting [Patience comes to those who wait.]|[NPC] Dust: WOW. [NPC] Dust: Barry said that?? [NPC] Dust: It's so simple, but so true... [NPC] Dust: I need to wake up and work on myself. [NPC] Dust: This Barry is such a wise man. [NPC] Dust: Oh gosh I think I'm tearing up...|
|In [[Barry HQ]]|[NPC] Dust: Barry is the only thing worth betting on.|
## Trivia
- While in [[Barry Center]], Dust and the other protestors have dialogue above their heads, such as "Barry bad!", "Go away Barry!", "Barry is trash.", "Go away Barry!", "Barry boooo!", and "Barry sucks!"
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Dust.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Edelis
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Edelis]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Talks about the Statue of the Hero.
Location: [[Scarleton Plaza]]
Coordinates: -61.5 107 -812.5
Description: Talks about the history of history of [[Scarleton]] and the Taika people.
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: -92.5 104 -821.5
Edelis is an [[NPC]] located in [[Scarleton]]. During the day, they stand outside the Statue of the Hero, and during the night they stay at their house.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; At Statue ([[Scarleton Plaza]])|[NPC] Edelis: This is the Statue of the Hero. I come here every day to pray. [NPC] Edelis: The legend says that a Hero arrived centuries ago, and helped us in times of need. [NPC] Edelis: Some of us still believe that the Hero will come back one day. [NPC] Edelis: Do you want me to tell the story? Select an option: [NO] [YES]|
|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Edelis: I could tell you were not very interested. [NPC] Edelis: I will go back to my prayers.|
|First Interaction; At Home|[NPC] Edelis: There are three different beliefs among the Taika. [NPC] Edelis: After we started learning magic centuries ago, most people stopped praying in the Cathedral. [NPC] Edelis: They were too busy to learn and create new spells, and even turned the Cathedral into an Academy. [NPC] Edelis: Those people will say that they believe in knowledge and magic. [NPC] Edelis: Some others still believe that it was our prayers that gave us our powers. [NPC] Edelis: They believe it was the Sky's plan to make us independent through Sulphur, and that the Sky is good and fair. [NPC] Edelis: And finally there are still very few people like me, who hold their faith in the Hero. [NPC] Edelis: The Hero was humble, yet they saved us all from doom.|
|Walking Home|[NPC] Edelis: I must pray at home, for it is late. [NPC] Edelis: The town may never sleep, but I do.|
|Walking to Statue ([[Scarleton Plaza]])|[NPC] Edelis: Rise and shine! Pray from dusk til dawn! [NPC] Edelis: Are you coming to pray with me?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|At Home|[NPC] Edelis: I'm trying to sleep.|
|At Home|[NPC] Edelis: It is very late.|
|At Statue ([[Scarleton Plaza]])|[NPC] Edelis: What do you think? Do you believe in the Hero too?|
|At Statue ([[Scarleton Plaza]])|[NPC] Edelis: Do you think the Hero was a man or a woman? There is so little information that remains...|
## History

|Mar 8, 2022|Added Edelis.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Edelis.|

# Elise
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Elise]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Grants the player the [[Rift Necklace]], after donating the [[Supreme Timecharm]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
Location: [[Wizard Tower]]
Coordinates: 44.5 77 100.5
Elise is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wizard Tower]]. She gives the player the [[Rift Necklace]] after donating the [[Supreme Timecharm]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Above Enchanting XXX (30) ([[Enchanting]])|[NPC] Elise: Stand back, I'm tinkering with a new necklace. [NPC] Elise: Looks like there isn't enough Magic left in this universe to complete my project. [NPC] Elise: I'll have to talk to Barry...|
|Below Enchanting XXX (30) ([[Enchanting]])|[NPC] Elise: Stand back, I'm tinkering with a new necklace. [NPC] Elise: It's very complicated! Only great enchanteresses may understand.|
|After receiving [[Rift Necklace]] in [[Rift Dimension]]|[NPC] Elise: Go away, I'm extremely busy. [NPC] Elise: I can't focus when blockheads keep interrupting me! Select an option: [You told me to come talk to you.]|
|Selecting [You told me to come talk to you.]|[NPC] Elise: Look buddy, I don't know who you think you are, but back off okay? [NPC] Elise: You're annoying me! Select an option: [Chocolate?] [Caramel?] [Ice Cream?]|
|Selecting [Chocolate?]|[NPC] Elise: What? What is that even supposed to mean?|
|Selecting [Ice Cream?]|[NPC] Elise: Wait, do you have some? Select an option: [No?]|
|Selecting [No?]|[NPC] Elise: Then go away!|
|Selecting [Caramel?]|[NPC] Elise: Wooooaaah. [NPC] Elise: If you know this keyword, it means there's a me out there who figured out how to power the necklace. [NPC] Elise: But since I never stepped into the portal... [NPC] Elise: It means that other me must be a future version of my past self, before Barry opened the portal. [NPC] Elise: I do NOT understand the multi-dimensional journey I must have taken to reach this point... Select an option: [Can I get the necklace?]|
|Selecting [Can I get the necklace?]|[NPC] Elise: * snaps back to reality * [NPC] Elise: Yes.|
|After obtaining the [[Rift Necklace]]|[NPC] Elise: I need some time to figure out how to empower the necklace... [NPC] Elise: You breathing down my neck does NOT help.|
|After obtaining the [[Rift Necklace]]|[NPC] Elise: Sure I haven't personally figured out the necklace... [NPC] Elise: ... but it's close enough to get credit for it! [NPC] Elise: Plus, I actually need to finish it to avoid a paradox!|
|After obtaining the [[Rift Necklace]]|[NPC] Elise: I'm ready to do whatever is needed to finish this work. [NPC] Elise: People are remembered by their greatest work, so everything else doesn't matter.|
### Removed
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None; [AHN] (This item or feature is exclusive to the Alpha Hypixel Network and will never be released on the main Hypixel server.) Exclusive|[NPC] Elise: Sorry, I'm busy tinkering.|

|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Elise from 43.5, 82, 68.675 ([[Coordinates]]) to 44.5, 77, 100.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 13, 2022|Added Elise.|

# Elise (Rift)
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, More info needed
[Elise]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Gives access to the [[Rift Gallery]].
Location: Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]])
Coordinates: -50.35 122 69.35
Description: Manages the [[Rift Gallery]].
Location: [[Rift Gallery]]
Coordinates: 9.875 55 76
Elise is an [[NPC]] first located in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]], before moving to the [[Rift Gallery]] and giving the [[Rift Necklace]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First time in the [[Rift Dimension]]|[NPC] Elise: Sorry, I'm busy right now. [NPC] Elise: I'm working on the next big thing!|
|After the first time in the Rift Dimension|[NPC] Elise: I finally did it! [NPC] Elise: Finally figured it out! [NPC] Elise: By combining the magic of the Rift with the rest of the universe... [NPC] Elise: ... I'm able to create the most powerful necklace ever devised. Select an option: [Can I have one?]|
|Selecting [Can I have one?]|[NPC] Elise: Of course! But there's one tiny hiccup... [NPC] Elise: In order to combine the magic, we will need to have the magic. [NPC] Elise: But I suppose for an adventurer of your caliber, traversing the weirdest parallel universe... [NPC] Elise: Avoiding being stuck forever... [NPC] Elise: Dodging giant, triple-stacked, INSTAKILLING temporal pillars... [NPC] Elise: Potentially fighting vampires... [NPC] Elise: Is NO problem at all? Select an option: [What do I need to do for the necklace?]|
|Selecting [What do I need to do for the necklace?]|[NPC] Elise: Very well. [NPC] Elise: First, bring me the Supreme Timecharm. [NPC] Elise: Despite the name, it's not the best one. [NPC] Elise: It's the first of like 8 Timecharms. [NPC] Elise: We'll need that before going any further.|
|Before obtaining the [[Supreme Timecharm]]|[NPC] Elise: Find the Supreme Timecharm somewhere in the Rift. [NPC] Elise: And only then can we move forward with the necklace.|
|After obtaining the Supreme Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: Woooooooaaahhhhhh! [NPC] Elise: You managed to get the Supreme Timecharm! [NPC] Elise: We need to secure it to safety. [NPC] Elise: Use the warp to the gallery. [NPC] Elise: I will meet you there.|
|After teleporting to the [[Rift Gallery]]|[NPC] Elise: This is the Rift Gallery! [NPC] Elise: The wizards managed to take control of this section of the dimension for use in my necklace project. [NPC] Elise: Secure Timecharms here every time you find one! [NPC] Elise: Place the Supreme Timecharm in the right slot of the gallery!|
|After securing the Supreme Timecharm, before claiming the [[Rift Necklace]]|[NPC] Elise: Marvelous! [NPC] Elise: I frankly did not expect that much from you. [NPC] Elise: Here is the Necklace.|
|After obtaining the Rift Necklace|[NPC] Elise: Okay... it might not seem incredible right now... [NPC] Elise: But there are two things about it! [NPC] Elise: First, it becomes way stronger the more Timecharms you secure in the Rift. [NPC] Elise: Second, the real power is OUTSIDE, in the "real" universe. [NPC] Elise: Come and see me in the Wizard Tower in the SkyBlock Hub. [NPC] Elise: Although, I might not be too kind out there. [NPC] Elise: Simply tell my outside self the keyword CARAMEL and I should understand. [NPC] Elise: Have fun!|
|The player last secured the Supreme Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: My mother was a wizard, while my father crafted beautiful jewelry. [NPC] Elise: Naturally, I decided to combine their talents!|
|After securing the [[MrahcemiT esrevrorriM]]|[NPC] Elise: Congratulations on obtaining the mrahcemiT esrevrorriM! [NPC] Elise: I've heard that one demands wits, a quality that cannot be gained easily.|
|The player last secured the mrahcemiT esrevorriM|[NPC] Elise: Numerous wizards have ventured into the Rift, but not all are from the world you and I come from. [NPC] Elise: Due to the Rift's nature, we can never be sure if they hail from the past or from a time yet to come.|
|After securing the [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]]|[NPC] Elise: You've collected another Timecharm! [NPC] Elise: As you journey deeper into the Rift, things maybe become more challenging. [NPC] Elise: My advice? Gather as much ф Rift Time as possible before proceeding further.|
|The player last secured the Chicken N Egg Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: Barry is truly one of the most skilled wizards I've ever met. [NPC] Elise: Too bad he's using his powers for all the wrong reasons. [NPC] Elise: Don't tell him I said that! [NPC] Elise: There's been enough complaints against him recently.|
|After securing the [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]]|[NPC] Elise: Barry was rather downcast before his first election. [NPC] Elise: But once elected, he discovered he could make a positive impact. [NPC] Elise: He REALLY wanted to become mayor again, to no avail. [NPC] Elise: I guess he found a way... [NPC] Elise: In any case, without his help, we couldn't have fixed the portal.|
|The player last secured the SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: Honestly, I've made my peace with leaving all the meaning of my life to the past. [NPC] Elise: If getting here was my purpose in life, then anything beyond that point would've been meaningless anyway. [NPC] Elise: At least now it's official. [NPC] Elise: But sometimes, when I'm alone in the Gallery... [NPC] Elise: ... I wish I knew what I was sacrificing by stepping into the breach.|
|After securing the [[Living Timecharm]]|[NPC] Elise: The Living Cave is not a place I'm fond of. [NPC] Elise: It's cold, isolated and utterly confounding. [NPC] Elise: I'm relieved you managed to secure the Timecharm from there!|
|The player last secured the Living Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: Best of luck battling the infection in the Colosseum. [NPC] Elise: I crossed the breach a little bit after Dante's defeat in our world. [NPC] Elise: While I can't be sure if the Slime was there "before" his demise, the resemblance is striking. [NPC] Elise: The wizards believe Shen employed Dante, or perhaps even created him! [NPC] Elise: Or did Shen find a way into the Rift before us? [NPC] Elise: Did he find Dante here?|
|After securing the [[Globulate Timecharm]]|[NPC] Elise: Unbelievable! [NPC] Elise: You truly vanquished Bacte? [NPC] Elise: Perhaps you're the one who can reach the Mountaintop...|
|The player last secured the Globulate Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: Have you encountered Julian? [NPC] Elise: He's been spotted in the Rift on several occasions. [NPC] Elise: While he may seem friendly, be cautious. [NPC] Elise: Those in his organization have committed questionable acts. [NPC] Elise: One doesn't reach his rank without crossing a few lines.|
|After securing the [[Vampiric Timecharm]]|[NPC] Elise: The vampires have bestowed their Timecharm upon you? [NPC] Elise: You must've made a powerful impression on their Coven.|
|The player last secured the Vampiric Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: The Rift holds countless more secrets. [NPC] Elise: We may never fully comprehend it, but your assistance has been invaluable so far! [NPC] Elise: I've heard rumors that the breach on top of the Mountain was made by a massive Dragon. [NPC] Elise: But hey, did the breach cause the Rift? [NPC] Elise: Or was the Rift just hanging around before we even had a way in? [NPC] Elise: Guess we might never figure that out...|
|After securing the [[Celestial Timecharm]]|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|
|The player last secured the Celestial Timecharm|[NPC] Elise: Nice job getting all the timecharms. [NPC] Elise: Your Rift Necklace is totally maxed out now. [NPC] Elise: Really appreciate the help with all this. [NPC] Elise: Drop by sometime, it gets pretty lonely out here. [NPC] Elise: Please.|
## Trivia
- Elise has a number in Roman numerals next to her name, referencing the amount of times the player has entered the [[Rift Dimension]].
  - This is also in the description of [[Rift Necklace]] and is updated with every visit.
  - If the player has never entered the Rift yet, her name is simply Elise.
  - If the player has entered the Rift 4,000 or more times, her name will become Elise TOO_HIGH_NUMBER.
- Elise's dialogue Return in 3-5 business days is a reference to the [[Wizard]]'s dialogue before the Rift update.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Elise.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Elise.|

Emissaries are [[NPC|NPCs]] scattered across the [[Dwarven Mines]] and also in the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
## Mechanics
Emissaries appear in a location once the player reach [[Commissions|Commissions Milestone I]]. They grant access to the [[Commissions|Commissions Menu]] that can otherwise be accessed through the [[King]], [[Royal Pigeon]] or [[Queen Mismyla]]‘s [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; Emissary Braum|[NPC] Emissary Braum: I am the King's favorite Emissary, which is why I'm the furthest away from him! [NPC] Emissary Braum: Commissions inside the Crystal Hollows are different from the ones in the Dwarven Mines. [NPC] Emissary Braum: Click me again to recieve your first set of Crystal Hollows commissions. [NPC] Emissary Braum: Once you complete them, come back to me!|
|First Interaction; Emissary Lissandra|[NPC] Emissary Lissandra: I'd advise against going ahead. [NPC] Emissary Lissandra: But, if you do, the current "King" has some tasks he would appreciate your help with. [NPC] Emissary Lissandra: I say King, but ever since Dulin returned, whispers are going around of him being the true king.|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Carlton None|[NPC] Emissary Carlton: The King has sent me to go shopping for I am his favorite emissary!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Carlton Completing|[NPC] Emissary Carlton: This will make me gain even more favor with the King!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Carlton Denying|[NPC] Emissary Carlton: How dare you reject me, the King's favorite emissary?!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Ceanna None|[NPC] Emissary Ceanna: The King's favorite emissary is here to request potatoes for the King's great feast!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Ceanna Completing|[NPC] Emissary Ceanna: Maybe the King will even announce me as his favorite at the feast!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Ceanna Denying|[NPC] Emissary Ceanna: I will inform the King about this disgrace!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Fraiser None|[NPC] Emissary Fraiser: I know what all the others say, but I am the King's true favorite emissary.|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Fraiser Completing|[NPC] Emissary Fraiser: As the King's true favorite only I know he secretly loves melon.|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Fraiser Denying|[NPC] Emissary Fraiser: The King will surely punish you for this disrespect!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Sisko None|[NPC] Emissary Sisko: I've heard I can find the best mushrooms here, and as the King's favorite emissary I will bring them to him!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Sisko Completing|[NPC] Emissary Sisko: My standing as the King's favorite emissary is secured as soon as I bring these back home!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Sisko Denying|[NPC] Emissary Sisko: You are going to regret this!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Wilson None|[NPC] Emissary Wilson: The King's least favorite vegetable is carrot, so he sent me, his favorite emissary, to make it better!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Wilson Completing|[NPC] Emissary Wilson: I'm sure that by giving him his least favorite vegetable, he'll make me his favoritest emissary!|
|[[The Garden]]; Emissary Wilson Denying|[NPC] Emissary Wilson: I'm not sure if the carrots would have worked anyways...|
## Trivia
- Emissary Sisko is most likely a reference to Benjamin Sisko, the protagonist of Star Trek: Deep Space Nine, who also held the title of Emissary to the Prophets.

Erihann is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wizard Tower]]. The player can imbue their [[Rift Prism]] to them for 200,000 [[Coins]] to consume it and gain [[Accessory Power|11 Accessory Power]] and free entry to the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Erihann: Oh.... [NPC] Erihann: Hi! [NPC] Erihann: I'm looking for a very specific artifact. [NPC] Erihann: You'll know when you find it.|
|Interacting while having [[Rift Prism]] in Inventory|[NPC] Erihann: The Rift Prism! [NPC] Erihann: It's very powerful, but sometimes... it fails. [NPC] Erihann: Basically, as the Rift is another dimension... [NPC] Erihann: ... sometimes it cannot check your accessory bag. [NPC] Erihann: It's a bunch of magic nonsense, but what matters is, I have the fix!|
|Imbuing|Erihann imbued your Rift Prism right into your spirit! This is accompanied by a cluster of dissonant Ghast Screams.|

Etc is an [[NPC]] in [[Dragontail]], and one of the three children of [[Chihai]]. Their siblings are [[Chak]] and [[Chuk]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Etc: I was born last.|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 1/4)|[NPC] Etc: Are you a Wizard? [NPC] Etc: Well you don't look like one, and you don't smell like one either. [NPC] Etc: In that case, I have nothing against you. I wish you a sulphuric day sir.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 2/4)|[NPC] Etc: Hello stranger. Are you looking to join our faction? [NPC] Etc: What a fantastic choice! [NPC] Etc: We have a range of motives far deeper and more interesting than those terrible fire mages. [NPC] Etc: Joining our faction is quite hard but I'm sure an incredible individual like you will manage!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 3/4)|[NPC] Etc: Sul-phur [NPC] Etc: Sulphur? [NPC] Etc: Me Sulphur.|

Farmer Rigby gives the player the [[Quests|First Harvest]] [[Quests|Quest]] to gather 10 [[Wheat]] to feed their sick [[Cow]], [[Shania]]. # Farmhand
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Farming, Quest giver
[Farmhand]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Back at the Barnyard ([[Quests]])
Description: Gives the player a quest to craft a [[Wheat Minion]].
Location: [[The Barn]]
Coordinates: 144 73 -240
The Farmhand is an [[NPC]] located in [[The Barn]]. They give the player a quest to craft a [[Wheat Minion]] followed by collecting various items for them from around the area.
## Quest
The Farmhand grants the player the [[Wheat Minion]] recipe when first speaking to him. Upon presenting him with a [[Wheat Minion|Wheat Minion I]], they reward the player with a [[Chicken Pet]], and teach them how to use it. Afterwards, the player is tasked with collecting other resources on [[The Barn]], with the Farmhand rewarding +10 [[Farming|Farming XP]] upon completion. After reaching [[Farming|Farming V (5)]], the player is rewarded with [[SkyBlock Levels|+5 SkyBlock XP]] and +300 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Dialogue

|-|-|

|Presenting the [[Wheat Minion]]|[NPC] Farmhand: Nice! I have a Tier X Wheat Minion of my own, maybe one day you can be like me! [NPC] Farmhand: While Minions are great, the best way to gather resources is by hand. [NPC] Farmhand: Take this Chicken Pet, it'll help you gather more crops!|
|After obtaining the [[Chicken Pet]], and before applying it|[NPC] Farmhand: In order to summon your Chicken Pet, you'll need to add it to your Pets Menu first. [NPC] Farmhand: Hold your Chicken Pet in your hand and right-click to put it in your Pets Menu!|
|After adding the [[Chicken Pet]] to the Pets Menu, and before summoning it|[NPC] Farmhand: Great! Now go to the SkyBlock Menu and and open up your Pets Menu. [NPC] Farmhand: From there, find your Chicken Pet and left-click to summon it!|
|After summoning the [[Chicken Pet]]|[NPC] Farmhand: That Chicken Pet gives you Farming Fortune, which gives you a chance to drop additional crops! [NPC] Farmhand: Try collecting some of the various crops on the island.|
|Random None|[NPC] Farmhand: The farm animals here provide a variety of resources. [NPC] Farmhand: Did you know that chickens drop eggs and cows can be milked?|
|Random None|[NPC] Farmhand: Collect wheat, carrots, potatoes, pumpkins, and melon to add to your Collection and unlock cool rewards!|
|Random None|[NPC] Farmhand: Those Fairy Souls scare off the animals.|
|Random Before Visiting [[Mushroom Desert]]|[NPC] Farmhand: Have you been to the Mushroom Desert? [NPC] Farmhand: You can find many more types of crops there!|
|Random Before Reaching Farming V (5) ([[Farming]])|[NPC] Farmhand: The entrance to the Mushroom Desert sits atop a hill to the north. [NPC] Farmhand: There you'll find resources only an experienced farmer can handle - cocoa beans, sugar cane, mushrooms, and cactus! # Fetchur
Aliases: Fetcher, Fletchur
Categories: Non-player characters, Dwarven Mines, Quest giver
[Fetchur]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tasks the player with bringing them a specific item each day in exchange for 2,500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] and 20,000 [[Coins]].
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 84 224 -118
Fetchur's Current Request
20 [[Mithril]] until in your time zone (ⓘ)
Next: 1 [[Firework Rocket]]
Fetchur is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. They ask players to find a specific item every day as a quest.
## Mechanics
All players get the same request each day, and it changes for everyone at the same time, regardless of whether the player delivered the item the previous day or not. The quest can only be completed once per day, and it resets every night at midnight [[Time Systems|Hypixel Server Time]], which is either 4 AM or 5 AM UTC depending on U.S. daylight saving time.
### Rewards
Upon giving Fetchur the correct amount of the requested item, the player receives 20,000 [[Coins]] and 2,500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]. The ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] bonus is affected by the Powder Buff [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM perk]]. Although not mentioned in the message, it is also affected by the [[2x Powder]] event. The player also receives 10 [[Bits]], multiplied by their [[Fame Ranks|Bits Multiplier]], as long as they have enough [[Bits]] available.
### Requests Cycle
The requested item cycles through a list of 12 different possible items. The cycle restarts each month with [[Stained Glass|Yellow Stained Glass]].
|Fetchur's Hint|Item|
|-|-|
|yellow and see through|20 Yellow Stained Glass ([[Stained Glass]])|
|circular and sometimes moves|1 [[Compass]]|
|expensive minerals|20 [[Mithril]]|
|useful during celebrations|1 [[Firework Rocket]]|
|hot and gives energy|1 [[Cheap Coffee]], [[Decent Coffee]] or [[Black Coffee]]|
|tall and can be opened|1 [[Iron Door]] or Wood Door ([[Wooden Door]])|
|brown and fluffy|3 [[Rabbit's Foot]]|
|explosive but more than usual|1 [[Superboom TNT]]|
|wearable and grows|1 [[Pumpkin]]|
|shiny and makes sparks|1 [[Flint and Steel]]|
|green and some dudes trade stuff for it|50 [[Emerald]]|
|red and soft|50 Red Wool ([[Wool]])|
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Daily Interaction|[NPC] Fetchur: hi i need your help maybe [NPC] Fetchur: im looking for some stuff, dont remember the name tbh [NPC] Fetchur: its/theyre [Fetchur's hint] [NPC] Fetchur: i need [quantity] pls|
|Without Item|[NPC] Fetchur: you didnt find my things yet? [NPC] Fetchur: its/theyre [Fetchur's hint] [NPC] Fetchur: i need [quantity] pls|
|Incorrect Item|[NPC] Fetchur: i didnt ask for this bro [NPC] Fetchur: why you giving me something completely different [NPC] Fetchur: people these days lol... [NPC] Fetchur: its/theyre [Fetchur's hint] [NPC] Fetchur: i need [quantity] pls|
|Wrong Amount|[NPC] Fetchur: um yea youre close [NPC] Fetchur: but wrong amount?? [NPC] Fetchur: its/theyre [Fetchur's hint] [NPC] Fetchur: i need [quantity] pls|
|Correct Item|[NPC] Fetchur: thanks thats probably what i needed [NPC] Fetchur: take some gifts!|
|Already Completed|[NPC] Fetchur: come back another time, maybe tmrw|
|Disabled; 20-21 Jan 2021; 14 Jul-21 Aug 2021|This NPC is temporarily disabled, sorry!|
|Disabled; 22 Jan 2021|i have been turned off for now, go away.|
## Trivia
- Completing Fetchur's quest for the first time grants the player the [[Achievements|"Helpful Hand" Achievement]].
- If the reward is claimed during a [[2x Powder]] event, the chat will still incorrectly state that only 2,500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] was given to the player.
- Fetchur will not accept the items if it's not exactly the requested amount, even if the player attempts to give more than what was requested.

# Finnegan
Aliases: Blooming Business, Blooming business, Candidate Finnegan, Finn, Mayor Finnegan, Minister Finnegan
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Finnegan Finnegan]
Also known as: Mayor Finnegan
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Pest Eradicator The duration of Pesthunter Phillip's ☘ Farming Fortune bonus is now 60m. Decreases the spawn cooldown of Pests by 20%.; GOATed Jacob's Farming Contest brackets include 10%; more players each.; Blooming Business All Garden Visitors reward Fine Flour and +10% Copper for accepting offers. Invites 5 unique visitors to your Garden and makes higher rarity visitors 20% more likely to show up.; Grand Feast Finnegan upgrades the Harvest Feast into the Grand Feast, making it lasts a full SkyBlock year. Also opens the Grand Bakery, where Kernels can be spent on more exclusive items.; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Finnegan!
Finnegan is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. His perks focus around [[Farming]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Finnegan may run for election with up to 4 possible perks.
  [[Finnegan]] Finnegan
1. Pest Eradicator: The duration of [[Pesthunter Phillip]]'s [[Farming Fortune]] bonus is now 60m. Decreases the spawn cooldown of [[Pests]] by 20%.
2. GOATed: [[Jacob's Farming Contest]] brackets include up to 10% more players each.
3. Blooming Business: [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]] will give out [[Fine Flour]] and appear more often. Additional visitors may visit your [[The Garden|Garden]]. Higher rarity Visitors are more likely to show up & will provide 10% more [[Copper]].
4. Grand Feast: Finnegan upgrades the [[Harvest Feast]] into the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]], making it last a full SkyBlock year. Also opens the [[Communal Stew|Grand Bakery]], where [[Kernels]] can be spent on more exclusive items.
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Finnegan: I'm Finnegan! [NPC] Finnegan: Jacob's brother-in-law and Trevor's cousin!! [NPC] Finnegan: It's a small world! [NPC] Finnegan: With big farms!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Finnegan: All this farming is making me hungry!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Mayor Finnegan: Back to farming I go! These PBs ain't gonna beat themselves!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Finnegan: Guess I'll have to go gather the crops myself...|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Finnegan: All of us are trappers at heart!|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Finnegan: I don't know if farming is for everyone, but everyone should give it a shot!|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Finnegan: Yes, I do have a PhD in Agriculture & Forestry and a Masters in Applied Mathematics.|
## Trivia
- Finnegan first joined a [[Mayor Election]] in year 241.
- Finnegan is [[Jacob]]'s brother-in-law and [[Trevor]]'s cousin.
  - This implies that either Jacob, or Finnegan or both of them are married, and that Finnegan and Trevor share grandparents.
- Finnegan's skin seems to have been created by a skyblock player called 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] Zelenuk_
## History

GOATed: Jacob's Farming Contests' brackets include 10% more players each.|

# Fisherman Gerald
Aliases: Fisherman, Gerald
Categories: Non-player characters, Fishing, Quest giver, NPCs with Epithets
[Fisherman Gerald]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Finding the Engine ([[Quests]])
Description: Introduces the player to the Ship Navigator ([[Ship]]) and allows them to fish up the [[Rusty Ship Engine]] to fix it.
Location: [[Fishing Outpost]]
Coordinates: 118.5 71 -32.5
Fisherman Gerald is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Fisherman's Hut]]. He introduces the player to the [[Captain Baha|Ship Navigator]] and allows players to fish up the [[Rusty Ship Engine]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] Below Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])|[NPC] Gerald: Hmm, you're not quite ready yet. [NPC] Gerald: Go talk to the Fishing Merchant in the Fishing Outpost, get geared up, and get fishin'! [NPC] Gerald: Come back once you've reached Fishing Skill V and I'll tell you how you can get to a new island!|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] First interaction above Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])|[NPC] Gerald: Keep the noise down, kid! [NPC] Gerald: If you want to learn about Fishing, go talk to my wife, Fisherwoman Enid. [NPC] Gerald: She's fishing a bit upstream. Once she's shown you the ropes, come back and talk to me!|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] After talking to [[Fisherwoman Enid]]|[NPC] Gerald: Hey there buddy, think you can help me out? Select an option: [What do you need?]|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] After selecting [What do you need?]|[NPC] Gerald: My buddy, Captain Baha, and I are just about done building a ship that can set sail to the Backwater Bayou. [NPC] Gerald: All that's left is to add the Rusty Ship Engine, but I tripped and dropped it into the pond here... [NPC] Gerald: Do you think you could fish it out for me? [NPC] Gerald: If you find it, bring it to Captain Baha so you can set sail!|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] Before fishing up the [[Rusty Ship Engine]]|[NPC] Gerald: Can you fish up the Rusty Ship Engine for me? [NPC] Gerald: It's somewhere in this pond here. [NPC] Gerald: Once you fish it out, you can set sail to the Backwater Bayou!|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] After fishing the [[Rusty Ship Engine]]|[NPC] Gerald: Ah! The Rusty Ship Engine! Perfect! [NPC] Gerald: Bring that to Captain Baha and he'll be able to help you set sail! [NPC] Gerald: He's behind the Fisherman's Hut, waiting by the Ship. [NPC] Gerald: Just leave out the part about me dropping it, would ya?|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] After bringing the [[Rusty Ship Engine]] to [[Captain Baha]]|[NPC] Gerald: Thanks for helping me find the Rusty Ship Engine, <username>! [NPC] Gerald: You can use the Ship Navigator to set sail to the Backwater Bayou! [NPC] Gerald: Safe travels!|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] Idle|[NPC] Gerald: Fishing is the family business. [NPC] Gerald: Enid and I are thrilled that our two children love it as much as we do!|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] Idle|[NPC] Gerald: I met Captain Baha when I was marooned on a distant pirate cove. [NPC] Gerald: He saved my skin then, and I'm forever grateful to him.|
|[[Fisherman's Hut]] Idle|[NPC] Gerald: I've only gone lava fishing a few times in my life. [NPC] Gerald: I prefer the open water. More peaceful, less Ghasts!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Fisherman Gerald: A Carrot King has been scaring off all the good fish, I need to deal with it before I lose more fish.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Fisherman Gerald: Holy Mackerel! You got me the whole nine yards.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Fisherman Gerald: S'alright, we are all busy some days.|
## Trivia
- Fisherman Gerald is married to [[Fisherwoman Enid]], their children are [[Gwynnie]] and [[Gavin]].
- Fisherman Gerald's dialogue mentions a distant pirate cove, which refers to a [[Fishing]] island that was once planned but has since been scrapped.

# Fisherwoman Enid
Aliases: Enid
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Fishing, NPCs with Epithets
[Fisherwoman Enid]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Fishing Tutorial ([[Quests]])
Description: Introduces the player to [[Fishing]], and gives out a starter Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]).
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 41.5 70 -22.25
Fisherwoman Enid is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]]. She introduces the player to [[Fishing]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Enid: Hello, darling! [NPC] Enid: Are you here to fish? Select an option: [Yes!]|
|After selecting [Yes!]|[NPC] Enid: Splendid! [NPC] Enid: Take my spare Fishing Rod and stand near the water. [NPC] Enid: This rod isn't that special, but it will get the job done for now!|
|Not claiming the Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]])|[NPC] Enid: Don't forget your Fishing Rod!|
|Before catching a fish|[NPC] Enid: To fish, cast your rod into the water and wait for a fish to bite!|
|After catching a fish|[NPC] Enid: Splendid! [NPC] Enid: And that's really how simple Fishing is! [NPC] Enid: This part of the Hub is a popular area for fishing because of the Fishing Outpost! [NPC] Enid: If you follow the river that flows under this bridge downstream, you'll find your way to it! [NPC] Enid: Fisherman Gerald will be happy to help you on your Fishing journey. Just tell him his wife sent you!|
|Before talking to [[Fisherman Gerald]]|[NPC] Enid: This part of the hub is a popular area for fishing because of the Fishing Outpost! [NPC] Enid: If you follow the river that flows under the bridge downstream, you'll find your way to it. [NPC] Enid: And tell Gerald I won't be home for dinner tonight! I'm tired of him making salmon.|
|After talking to [[Fisherman Gerald]]|[NPC] Enid: If you like fishin', you'll love the Backwater Bayou! [NPC] Enid: You should go there sometime soon!|
|If you've visited the [[Backwater Bayou]]|[NPC] Enid: Using Bait is important to get better results! [NPC] Enid: My favorite kind is Dark Bait because I prefer fishing at night. [NPC] Enid: Angler Angus knows a lot more about Bait than I do. I think he's fishing somewhere along this river if you're interested in learning more.|
|If you've visited the [[Backwater Bayou]]|[NPC] Enid: I know that Gavin uses /scg instead of looking in his Fishing Skill menu. [NPC] Enid: He thinks he's being sneaky, but everyone knows about it. [NPC] Enid: Little does he know, I use it too!|
|If you've visited the [[Backwater Bayou]]|[NPC] Enid: The Backwater Bayou has all sorts of wild Sea Creatures native to the area. [NPC] Enid: I've heard stories of Frog Man, but I've never quite worked out if he's a frog or a man. [NPC] Enid: Perhaps he is both, perhaps neither.|

# Foxy
Aliases: Candidate Foxy, Foxy the Event Girl, Mayor Foxy, Minister Foxy
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Foxy Foxy]
Also known as: Mayor Foxy
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Sweet Benevolence Earn +30% more Candy, Gifts and Chocolate from duplicate Rabbits during their respective events.; Extra Event Schedules an extra {0} event during the year.; A Time for Giving Party Chests and Party Gifts can be obtained while this event is active.; Chivalrous Carnival Schedules a Carnival in the Hub that is active throughout the entire year.; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Foxy!
Foxy the Event Girl is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. Her perks focus around [[Events]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Foxy may run for election with up to 4 possible perks.
  [[Foxy]] Foxy the Event Girl
1. Sweet Benevolence: Earn +30% more [[Spooky Festival|Candy]], [[Gifts]] and [[Chocolate]] from duplicate [[Chocolate Rabbits|Rabbits]] during their respective events.
2. Extra Event: Schedules an extra event during the year, out of: [[Spooky Festival]], [[Mining Fiesta]], and [[Fishing Festival]].
3. A Time for Giving: [[Party Chest|Party Chests]] and [[Party Gift|Party Gifts]] can be obtained while this event is active.
4. [[Carnival|Chivalrous Carnival]]: Schedules a [[Carnival]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] that is active throughout the entire year.
## Extra Event
The Extra Event perk causes one additional event to be scheduled at some point during the year Foxy is elected. This event will be another Candidate's Mayoral Perk. Extra Event may be one of the following: [[Spooky Festival]], [[Mining Fiesta]], or [[Fishing Festival]].
For Mining Fiesta and Fishing Festival, this is only a single 2 hour 20 minute or 1 hour long event, respectively.
### List of Extra Events in the Past
Foxy's extra events are recorded with Historical Election Results. You may refer to [[Mayor Election|Mayor Election#Previous Elections]].
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Foxy: Yooooooooooooooo. It's me!! FOXY!!! [NPC] Foxy: This is so boring!!!!!! [NPC] Foxy: Let's turn the heat up, it’s party time! [NPC] Foxy: Why not just schedule an extra event??|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Foxy: Yoooo! FOXY is in TOWN!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Mayor Foxy: Let's get this party started!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Foxy: You are soooo boring.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Foxy: Can we get 50 MILLION VOTES?!?|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Foxy: Vote for me if you like fun!|
## Trivia
- Foxy's skin is a reference to the video game Tetris.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 3, 2024|Revamped Foxy's mayor perks.|
|Jun 4, 2024|Sweet Benevolence perk now grants +30% Chocolate from duplicate rabbits during [[Hoppity's Hunt]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Foxy.|
|Dec 13, 2022|Fixed an issue with Foxy and the [[Spooky Festival]] preventing the event from playing out properly.|
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Sweet Tooth and Benevolence perks merged into Sweet Benevolence. A Time For Giving and Chivalrous Carnival perks Added.|

# Fragilis
Aliases: Block Guide, Block guide, Handy Block Guide
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines
[Fragilis]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to access their Handy Block Guide.
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 88 199 -108
Fragilis is an [[NPC]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] that gives information about blocks in the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]] among others locations.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
- This menu can be opened through /handyblockguide or /hbg.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Fragilis: Y'know now that we are getting deeper and deeper a lot of these blocks are getting harder to mine! [NPC] Fragilis: Luckily I have a handy guide right here for you!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Fragilis: Hey you! Can you bring me the hardest block you can mine?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Fragilis: Cactus? I'm sure you could do better, but I'll take it for now.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Fragilis: Just as I thought...I am talking to a softie!|
## Trivia
- The name Fragilis is based on the word "fragile", itself being the etymological root and the Latin translation of the word.
- Fragilis has the same skin as [[Three Bears|Keith]], one of the [[Three Bears]] before they transform.
- Fragilis does not mention all blocks mineable in certain areas (for example Diamond Blocks in [[Mines of Divan]] or [[Cobblestone]]).
- Some entries within the Handy Block Guide lack a [[Block Strength]] :
  - For [[Ores]] and [[Dwarven Metals]], this could be due to an omission.
  - For [[Blocks]], this could be due to the varying mechanics of the breaking system as they seem to lack this property, instead using the Vanilla mechanics of Minecraft [1].
## History

|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Fragilis.|
|May 28, 2024|0.20.2|Corrected the Breaking Power value of Opal from -1 to 7.|

Frankie is an [[NPC]] who first appears in the [[Barry Center]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They start out as a protestor against [[Barry]], but after the player handles their complaints, they move to [[Barry HQ]] and become a worker.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Frankie: Barry is a terrible leader! [NPC] Frankie: He doesn't want to hear us out!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Frankie: Barry is hiding from us! [NPC] Frankie: He doesn't want to face reality!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Frankie: Someone promised donuts when coming to this protest. [NPC] Frankie: And I don't see ANY DONUT! [NPC] Frankie: Screw Barry!|
|After first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Frankie: I'm lagging!! All the time! [NPC] Frankie: It wasn't like that with the last Prime Minister!! [NPC] Frankie: It's so frustrating!!!! I should tell Barry about Frankie's complaints...|
|After telling Barry about Frankie's complaints|[NPC] Frankie: I'm lagging!! All the time! [NPC] Frankie: It wasn't like that with the last Prime Minister!! [NPC] Frankie: It's so frustrating!!!! Select the appropriate Barry rhetoric: ① [Are you perhaps using a VPN?] ② [You might be on a bad proxy.] ③ [Maybe you are downloading something?]|
|Selecting ① [Are you perhaps using a VPN?]|[NPC] Frankie: No, I'm lagging even with my VPN turned off.|
|Selecting ③ [Maybe you are downloading something?]|[NPC] Frankie: Not really, that can't be. [NPC] Frankie: Both Grandiose Games and Vapor are closed.|
|Selecting ② [You might be on a bad proxy.]|Frankie left. Frankie joined. [NPC] Frankie: Oh wow! [NPC] Frankie: That fixed it. [NPC] Frankie: Thank you Barry! And you too, [Player's Name]!|
|In [[Barry HQ]]|[NPC] Frankie: One day will come when the world will be cured from lag. [NPC] Frankie: If anyone can do it, it's Barry.|
## Trivia
- Their dialogue refers to game distribution services Steam as Vapor and Epic Games Store as Grandiose Games.
- While in [[Barry Center]], Frankie and the other protestors have dialogue above their heads, such as "Barry bad!", "Go away Barry!", "Barry is trash.", "Go away Barry!", "Barry boooo!", and "Barry sucks!"

# Generow
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Events, Season of Jerry
[Generow]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to access Gifting Milestones.
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
Coordinates: -10.5 76 93.5
Generow is an [[NPC]] located in [[Jerry's Workshop]] only in the month of December. They can be talked to to see [[Gifts|Gifting]] Milestones.
## Gifting Milestones
Generow grants the [[Player]] rewards based on how many gifts they've given to unique Players. When holding a [[Gifts|Gift]], players will see a green ✉ above players they have not gifted to before and a red ✉ above those they have already gifted to.
## Rewards
The cumulative rewards at Level XXV (25) are:
- 40 [[North Stars]]
- 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Ice Essence]]
- 360 (5 stacks plus 40) [[Frozen Bait]]
- 8 [[Gift of Learning]]
- [[Einary's Red Hoodie]]
- [[Expensive Toy]]
- Gift of Giving [[SkyBlock Levels|Emblem]] ✉
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Generow: Oh, hello there! [NPC] Generow: You look like someone who appreciates the finer points of gifting, am I right? Select an option: [Who are you?]|
|Selecting [Who are you?]|[NPC] Generow: Ah, where are my manners? [NPC] Generow: I'm Generow, the coolest gift-loving penguin you'll ever meet! [NPC] Generow: I live for two things: receiving gifts and gifting others. [NPC] Generow: But I must admit, the latter warms my feathers even more! Select an option: [What are you doing here?]|
|Selecting [What are you doing here?]|[NPC] Generow: Well, you see, this December is special. [NPC] Generow: I've come to reward those who share the joy of gifting! [NPC] Generow: Gift enough different players, and you'll earn yourself something extraordinary! Select an option: [Tell me more about these rewards.]|
|Selecting [Tell me more about these rewards.]|[NPC] Generow: Ah, eager to know, are we? [NPC] Generow: The more players you gift, the better your reward. [NPC] Generow: It's all about spreading the cheer, you see. [NPC] Generow: Check this out!|
|After hitting a milestone|[NPC] Generow: (gifts} Unique Gifts! Wow! [NPC] Generow: Click to claim your reward!|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Added Generow.|

# Gimley
Categories: Non-player characters, Dwarven Mines
[Gimley]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Likes to burp, along with the other NPCs in the [[Dwarven Tavern]].
Location: [[Dwarven Tavern]]
Coordinates: 29.5 202.281 -151.5
Gimley is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Dwarven Tavern]], who only says "burp"-type phrases.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Gimley: Burp|
|None|[NPC] Gimley: Buurp|
|None|[NPC] Gimley: Buuuuurp|
|None|[NPC] Gimley: Buuuuuuuuuuuuuurp|
|None|[NPC] Gimley: BURP|
|None|[NPC] Gimley: BUURRPPP|
|None|[NPC] Gimley: ‎*Inception BRRRAAWWWWWBRBRBB noises*|
|Condition|Dialogue|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Gimley: bUuUrP!?!?|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting|[NPC] Gimley: BuRRrP Burp!|
|[[The Garden]] Declining|[NPC] Gimley: BURP BURP!?!|
## Trivia
- Gimley is likely named after Gimli the Dwarf from J.R.R. Tolkien's Lord of the Rings series.
- After speaking to Gimley, or one of the others in the [[Dwarven Tavern|Tavern]], typing "burp" in public chat will cause [[Hornum]] to reward the player with a [[Dwarven Tankard]].
  - Typing in party chat, guild chat, or private chat will not work.

# Gladiator
Categories: Non-player characters, Player Versus Player
[Gladiator]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Used to run the [[Colosseum]].
Location: [[Wilderness]]
Coordinates: 123.5 79 165.5
[Metadata]
ID: EVENT_MASTER
The Gladiator is an [[NPC]] in the [[Wilderness]] that used to run the [[Colosseum]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Gladiator: Welcome to the Colosseum! [NPC] Gladiator: Oh...wait. Nevermind. [NPC] Gladiator: Here, you can join me in drinking away your sorrows.|
|First Interaction; 0.1–0.17|[NPC] Gladiator: Welcome to the Colosseum! [NPC] Gladiator: Here you can prove your fighting skills to everyone.|
|None; 0.1–0.17|[NPC] Gladiator: Welcome to the Colosseum! [NPC] Gladiator: The arena is temporarily closed for renovations!|
## Trivia
- The Gladiator is the replacement for the Event Master. Even though it was replaced, the welcome message still refers to the Gladiator as the Event Master.
- Before they were disabled, the Gladiator could be interacted with to enter the queue for 1v1s versus other players.
- Gladiator's skin, originally titled "Oscar of Astora", was created by PixeloveR on Planet Minecraft.

# Grandma Wolf
Aliases: Grandma Wolf (disambiguation), Kill Combo
Categories: Non-player characters
[Grandma Wolf]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]].
Location: [[Grandma's House]]
Coordinates: -281.5 122 -190.5
Grandma Wolf is an [[NPC]] located on the top floor of [[Grandma's House]] in the [[Spider's Den]]. She grants the player the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], and upgrades it from [[Rarity|COMMON]] up to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: Oh, <player>! I'm having a conundrum! There's so much to kill, yet so little time. [NPC] Grandma Wolf: I've added the Grandma Wolf Pet to your Pets Menu. [NPC] Grandma Wolf: The Grandma Wolf is particularly adept at combos. The more mobs you kill in quick succession, the better! [NPC] Grandma Wolf: If you reach high enough Bestiary Milestones, I will increase the rarity of this pet! [NPC] Grandma Wolf: The pet does not need to be spawned for combos to work! Happy hunting!|
|Upgrading Pet ([[Grandma Wolf Pet]]) to UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: There you go! I've upgraded your Grandma Wolf Pet to Uncommon! [NPC] Grandma Wolf: Come back when you've reached Bestiary Milestone X to upgrade it to Rare!|
|Upgrading Pet ([[Grandma Wolf Pet]]) to RARE ([[Rarity]])|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: There you go! I've upgraded your Grandma Wolf Pet to Rare! [NPC] Grandma Wolf: Come back when you've reached Bestiary Milestone XV to upgrade it to Epic!|
|Upgrading Pet ([[Grandma Wolf Pet]]) to EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: There you go! I've upgraded your Grandma Wolf Pet to Epic! [NPC] Grandma Wolf: Come back when you've reached Bestiary Milestone XX to upgrade it to Legendary!|
|Upgrading Pet ([[Grandma Wolf Pet]]) to LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: There you go! I've upgraded your Grandma Wolf Pet to Legendary! [NPC] Grandma Wolf: I'm afraid that's all I can do for you. Thank you for helping this little old Grandma!|
|Pet ([[Grandma Wolf Pet]]) is Already LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: I've already upgraded your pet to the maximum tier! Leave this little old Grandma alone!|
|Losing Pet ([[Grandma Wolf Pet]])|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: I'm afraid you don't have a Grandma Wolf Pet! Leave this little old Grandma alone!|
|Without [[Bestiary]] Requirement|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: I will upgrade your Grandma Wolf Pet to [rarity] when you reach Bestiary Milestone #.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: My little child, you don't look so good. You should eat something. I have an idea, give me some ingredients and I'll make you a nice soup.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: I will make you a meal that you won't forget!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Grandma Wolf: You denied Grandma Wolf's request. How dare you treat your granny like that.|
## Trivia
- Grandma Wolf's appearance is a reference to the fairy tale Little Red Riding Hood, where a wolf eats the protagonist's grandmother and dresses as her.
- Grandma Wolf is likely related to [[Grandpa Wolf]].
  - Both of them start their dialogue by introducing their respective conundrums to the player.
- There used to be a bug where spamming or holding right-click would upgrade the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]] for free all the way up to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
- You can have multiple rarities of this pet at once by buying the higher rarity from [[Rusty]] instead of having Grandma Wolf upgrade it to free, although there isn't really any benefit to this as it won't give additional pet score.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Grandma Wolf.|

# Grandpa Wolf
Aliases: Corpse Milestone, Frozen Corpse Milestone, Frozen Corpse Milestones
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Grandpa Wolf]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to Frozen Corpse Milestones ([[Frozen Corpses]]).
Location: [[Grandpa Wolf's Cave]]
Coordinates: -55.5 125 331.5
Grandpa Wolf is an [[NPC]] located in [[Grandpa Wolf's Cave]] within the [[Glacite Tunnels]]. He unlocks access to [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse Milestones]].
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Oh, <player>! I'm having a conundrum! There's so many corpses scattered around the Glacite Mineshafts, and so little time! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Tell you what, would you be willing to help me gather them all for me? I'll reward you handsomely! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: ...So long as I get to keep the corpses. Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Initially selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Awesome! Okay, stand still real quick... [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: *sniff sniff* [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Okay, now I'm familiar with your scent! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Using my amazing power of smell, I'll track you as you move through the Glacite Mineshafts. [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: ... [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: You know what the Glacite Mineshafts are, right? Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Initially selecting [No]|[NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Oh, that's okay. [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: My wife's not gonna be happy. [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Been out here for months without so much as a scrap to show for it... [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: You know, you're starting to look kinda tasty...|
|Selecting [Yes] after asking what Glacite Mineshafts are|[NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Great, the look on your face had me worried for a second! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Anyways, whenever you loot a Frozen Corpse, I'll come soon after to retrieve it. [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Come talk to me and I'll show you how many corpses we, the dream team, have gathered together! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: After reaching certain Frozen Corpse Milestones I'll even give you some useful stuff from my stash! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Here, take a look!|
|Selecting [No] after asking what Glacite Mineshafts are|[NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Hmm, maybe you're not ready then, yet. [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Glacite Mineshafts sometimes pop up while you're mining in the Glacite Tunnels. [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: If you enter one, you'll fine Frozen Corpses strewn throughout the place - they're all that's left of some unfortunate dwarves from long ago! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Though they're not very talkative fellows on account of being dead 'n all, you can still have a rummage through their backpacks for some loot! [NPC] Grandpa Wolf: Now, why don't you go and find a Glacite Mineshafts or two, and then come talk to me again when you're ready for us to partner up?|
## Tips
- Players can use /viewfrozencorpsemilestones to access the milestone menu remotely.
- Players should unlock and max [[Heart of the Mountain|Gifts from the Departed perk]] in [[Heart of the Mountain]] before progressing this milestone.
## Trivia
- The total cost of keys for maxed Frozen Corpse milestone is .
- Grandpa Wolf is likely related to [[Grandma Wolf]].
  - Both of them start their dialogue by introducing their respective conundrums to the player.
- The [[German Wolf Skin]] uses the same head as Grandpa Wolf.
- The /viewfrozencorpsemilestones command can be used even without access to the [[Glacite Tunnels]].

# Grelius
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Grelius]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Guards the [[Throne Room]], along with [[Krevius]].
Location: Community Center ([[Community Center (Scarleton)]])
Coordinates: -131.5 101 -755.5
Description: Appears here during the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) to help the Apprentice ([[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)]]).
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -528.05 104 -920.77
Description: Appears here during the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) to discuss stolen [[Sulphur]].
Location: [[Mage Council]]
Coordinates: -119.5 82 -758.5
Description: Appears at the end of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]].
Location: [[The Wasteland]]
Coordinates: -376.5 117 -976.5
Grelius is an [[NPC]] and guard to [[Queen Nyx]], who primarily guards the [[Throne Room]] with [[Krevius]]. They also briefly appear in other places on the [[Crimson Isle]], twice during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]] and once at the end of both [[Factions|Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questlines]].
## Quests
Grelius prevents the player from entering the [[Throne Room]] until you present a [[Letter of Recommendation]] during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]].
Later in this questline, they appear with [[Krevius]] and [[Captain Ahone]] in the [[Burning Desert]] when you call Captain Ahone about the [[Apprentice (Crimson Isle)|Apprentice]]. They also appear with the two in the [[Mage Council]] when discussing stolen [[Sulphur]].
At the end of both [[Factions|Faction]] quests, they appear in the [[Forgotten Skull]] with [[Krevius]], [[Captain Ahone]], [[Elle]], [[Ugo]], and two [[Piglin Guard|Piglin Guards]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|With a [[Letter of Recommendation]]|[NPC] Krevius: WHY ARE YOU HERE? [NPC] Grelius: WHY ARE YOU HERE? Select an option: [I'm here to cause trouble, of course] [I have a letter of recommendation]|
|Selecting [I have a letter of recommendation]|[NPC] Krevius: FINE, YOU MAY PASS! [NPC] Grelius: FINE, YOU MAY PASS!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Krevius: DO YOU WANT TO BE PULVERIZED? [NPC] Grelius: DO YOU WANT TO BE PULVERIZED?|
|None|[NPC] Krevius: WHAT DO YOU WANT? [NPC] Grelius: WHAT DO YOU WANT?|
|None|[NPC] Krevius: WE HAVE OUR EYES ON YOU. [NPC] Grelius: WE HAVE OUR EYES ON YOU.|
## Trivia
- If the player selects the [I'm here to cause trouble, of course] option, they will be killed, with the death message: ☠ You got pulverised by Krevius and Grelius.
