# Gris
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Gris]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Barbarian Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Barbarian Training ([[Quests]]); Recover Sulphur ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Tasks the player with obtaining a [[Heavy Pearl]] during the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]).
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -684.5 124 -828.5
Description: Rewards the player with 150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation ([[Factions]]) and 200,000 [[Coins]] after handing in a [[Heavy Pearl]].
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -686 116 -832
Gris is an [[NPC]] located in one of the [[Dragontail]] houses. During the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], they task the player with obtaining a [[Heavy Pearl]] from [[The Matriarch]].
## Quest
After giving a [[Trophy Fish|Skeleton Fish]] and [[Trophy Fish|Flyfish]] to [[Lasea]] as part of the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], the player is tasked with speaking to Gris, who asks for them to collect a [[Heavy Pearl]] from [[The Matriarch]]. After obtaining one, Gris can be then be found inside the house and down the ladder. Providing the [[Heavy Pearl]] then rewards the player with [[Factions|150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]] and 200,000 [[Coins]]. The player must then speak to [[Chief Scorn]] to continue the quest.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before starting the quest|[NPC] Gris: There is no time... [NPC] Gris: I need to prepare.|
|Starting the quest|[NPC] Gris: Ahhhh! [NPC] Gris: Don't kill me!!!! [NPC] Gris: ... [NPC] Gris: Excuse me. You really scared me! [NPC] Gris: I'm not a gambler, but if I had to bet on a faction to win this war, I wouldn't bet on us. [NPC] Gris: Soon enough I'll barricade myself in my basement. [NPC] Gris: I have enough provisions to last at least 7 months. [NPC] Gris: That's not enough...no that's far from enough. [NPC] Gris: What I need the most right now is a Heavy Pearl. [NPC] Gris: If you bring one to me I'll reward you handsomely. [NPC] Gris: There is only one way to obtain Heavy Pearls. [NPC] Gris: First you have to go down in that giant cave below the Volcano. [NPC] Gris: From there you need to enter the cave that goes North-West. [NPC] Gris: Inside that cave lives very rare creatures called Hellwisps. [NPC] Gris: If you keep going down and down, you'll find another cave, which leads to The Matriarch. [NPC] Gris: That creature is much larger than the other ones and has been there for decades, if not centuries. [NPC] Gris: To get a Heavy Pearl, you need to let the Matriarch eat you alive. [NPC] Gris: Once you are inside its stomach, you can steal a few pearls. [NPC] Gris: Then all you have to do is escape before the acid kills you, I think there is a way but I'm not so sure how. [NPC] Gris: You have to get the pearls on your first try because the Matriarch won't eat you again. [NPC] Gris: Usually it takes at least a day before she forgets your scent and eats you again. [NPC] Gris: Please bring me back one of those Heavy Pearls.|
|Before bringing a [[Heavy Pearl]]|[NPC] Gris: I think the best way to get out is for The Matriarch to spit you out, but I'll admit I don't know how.|
|Before bringing a [[Heavy Pearl]]|[NPC] Gris: Heavy Pearls have many, many properties.|
|After bringing a [[Heavy Pearl]]|[NPC] Gris: Eeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeek! [NPC] Gris: You almost gave me a heart attack!!! [NPC] Gris: Oh, you managed to get a Heavy Pearl! [NPC] Gris: Now that you mention it, I remember how to escape the belly of the Matriarch! [NPC] Gris: All you have to do it punch its uvula! [NPC] Gris: In a way they are not so different than us. [NPC] Gris: Thank you very much, here take these Coins. +200,000 Coins!|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Gris: I'd rather you leave now.|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Gris: I don't really trust anyone.|

# Guber
Aliases: Guper
Categories: Non-player characters, Racing, Quest giver, Quest Rewards, Quests
[Guber]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player rewards for completing the [[End Race]].
Location: [[The End]]
Coordinates: -494.5 121 -241.5
Guber is an [[NPC]] located in [[The End]]. They let the player compete in the [[End Race]].
## End Race
Stepping on the pressure plate next to Guber will begin the [[End Race]]. The player must travel to the other side of the island and reach the checkpoint before returning back to the start to complete the race.
|Action|Message|
|-|-|
|Starting a race|THE END RACE Race started! Good luck!|
|Finishing a race|THE END RACE Race finished in ##:##.###!|
|Finishing a race (new personal best)|THE END RACE Race finished in ##:##.###! PERSONAL BEST!|
|Time to Beat|Rewards|
|-|-|
|Under 3 minutes (180 seconds)|8 [[Silent Pearl]]|
|Under 2 minutes (120 seconds)|[[Enderman Mask]]|
|Under 80 seconds|[[Nope the Fish]]|
|Under 48 seconds|[[Pig's Foot]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Standing on the plate early|[NPC] Guber: Hey you! Over here! Come talk to me! I'll teach you about racing!|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Guber: The End is a large island... my favorite hobby is racing across it! [NPC] Guber: My best time is pretty fast, you probably will never beat me. But you can try! [NPC] Guber: To complete the race, you'll need to reach the opposite side of the island and come all the way back. [NPC] Guber: Start the race by walking over the pressure plate. If you finish in under 3 minutes, I'll reward you for it! Good luck!|
|Before completing the 3 minute race|[NPC] Guber: Give it a go! Can you finish the race in 3 minutes or less?|
|Before completing the 3 minute race|[NPC] Guber: Run over the pressure plate to start racing!|
|During the 3 minute race|[NPC] Guber: What are you talking to me for? Get back in the race!|
|During the 3 minute race|[NPC] Guber: Run across The End to the opposite side of the island to complete the race!|
|Completing the race in under 3 minutes|[NPC] Guber: Not bad, [player]! [NPC] Guber: But you can get a much quicker time if you use the items that I do. [NPC] Guber: Take these Silent Pearls, they allow you to teleport without angering Endermen. [NPC] Guber: Try again, but this time come back in 2 minutes or less!|
|Before completing the 2 minute race|[NPC] Guber: Keep it up! Can you finish the race in 2 minutes or less?|
|Before completing the 2 minute race|[NPC] Guber: Run over the pressure plate to start racing!|
|During the 2 minute race|[NPC] Guber: You shouldn't be talking to me if you want to beat 2 minutes!|
|Completing the race in under 2 minutes|[NPC] Guber: You're getting much faster! [NPC] Guber: Have this Enderman Mask! I wear it sometimes to make me feel like an Enderman. [NPC] Guber: See if you can beat 80 seconds!|
|Before completing the 80 second race|[NPC] Guber: Keep at it! Can you finish the race in 80 seconds or less?|
|Before completing the 80 second race|[NPC] Guber: Run over the pressure plate to start racing!|

# Guildford
Categories: Non-player characters, UI Subpages, Racing
[Guildford]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Dungeon Hub Racing ([[Quests]])
Description: Allows the player to compete in the Dungeon Hub Races ([[Dungeon Hub Race]]).
Location: [[Dungeon Hub]]
Coordinates: -18.5 86 4.5
Guildford is an [[NPC]] in the [[Dungeon Hub]]. They let the player compete in the [[Dungeon Hub Race]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Guildford: I'm Guildford! Yes, that Guildford. I'm sure you've heard of me! [NPC] Guildford: There is some fantastic scenery around this land. I like to race back and forth! [NPC] Guildford: You can speak to me any time and I'll set up a race with the settings you choose. [NPC] Guildford: If you do well in the races I will reward you!|
|Completing Objective|[NPC] Guildford: Well done, [player].|
## Dungeon Hub Race
Stepping on the [[Light Weighted Pressure Plate|Gold Pressure Plate]] next to Guildford will begin the [[Dungeon Hub Race]]. The player must travel to the destination and reach the checkpoint to complete the race. Settings can be toggled to modify the race.

# Gulliver
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Events, Season of Jerry, Quests, Quest giver, Racing
[Gulliver]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Chicken Racing ([[Quests]])
Description: Allows the player to participate in the [[Chicken Race]].
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
Coordinates: 68.5 105 33.5
Gulliver is an [[NPC]] located in [[Jerry's Workshop]]. They give unique items for each checkpoint the player can reach.
## Chicken Race
Players can interact with Gulliver to start the Chicken Race. It gives rewards on different checkpoints of the race.
Players will have to go through multiple hoops to get to the checkpoints. Keep in mind that the hoops are quite well camouflaged. Turning particles to max in settings can make them easier to see. Every so often there will be a hoop with green particles, these hoops give extra time for the race.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Introduction|[NPC] Gulliver: Bukawk. Coot Coot Cluck, Puk Puk Pukaaak. [NPC] Gulliver: Buk bawk bakok Cluckpoint 1 puk pock < 33s? [NPC] Gulliver: Puk Puk Pukaaak.|
|Completing Cluckpoint 1|[NPC] Gulliver: Bukawk. Coot Coot Cluck, Puk Puk Pukaaak. [NPC] Gulliver: Cluck cluck! Bacawk! Red Gift bakok. [NPC] Gulliver: Buk bawk bakok Cluckpoint 2 puk pock < 52s? [NPC] Gulliver: Puk Puk Pukaaak.|
|Completing Cluckpoint 2|[NPC] Gulliver: Bukawk. Coot Coot Cluck, Puk Puk Pukaaak. [NPC] Gulliver: Cluck cluck! Bacawk! Gift Compass bakok. [NPC] Gulliver: Buk bawk bakok Cluckpoint 3 puk pock < 68s? [NPC] Gulliver: Puk Puk Pukaaak.|
|Completing Cluckpoint 3|[NPC] Gulliver: Bukawk. Coot Coot Cluck, Puk Puk Pukaaak. [NPC] Gulliver: Cluck cluck! Bacawk! Frozen Chicken bakok. [NPC] Gulliver: Buk bawk bakok Cluckpoint 4 puk pock < 104s? [NPC] Gulliver: Puk Puk Pukaaak.|
|Completing Cluckpoint 4|[NPC] Gulliver: Bukawk. Coot Coot Cluck, Puk Puk Pukaaak. [NPC] Gulliver: Cluck cluck! Bacawk! Jingle Bells bakok. [NPC] Gulliver: Puk Puk Pukaaak paaak.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Already Claimed Rewards|[NPC] Gulliver: Bak bawk bakok!|
|Already Claimed Rewards|[NPC] Gulliver: Coot Coot Cluck, Puk Puk Pukaaak!|
## Trivia
- Gulliver's chicken has a 0.3% chance to drop a [[Cluck the Fish]] instead of an egg.
- If you miss a hoop, you won’t be able to go through the next hoop until you go through the missed hoop.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Start Racing Before First Interaction|[NPC] Gustave: Hey! Come talk to me over here! I'll tell you all about racing!|
|Before exploring each island|[NPC] Gustave: There's nothing like island racing! [NPC] Gustave: Come back when you have unlocked the rest of The Park if you want to give it a try!|
|First interaction after exploring each island|[NPC] Gustave: There's nothing like island racing! [NPC] Gustave: I've traveled many islands and this is my favorite to race on. Want to try? [NPC] Gustave: To complete the race, you'll need to reach the opposite side of the island AND come back all the way back. [NPC] Gustave: Start the race by walking over the pressure plate. If you finish in under 2 minutes, I'll reward you for it! Good luck!|
|Before Completing First Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: Run over the pressure plate to start racing! Run up to the opposite side of the island AND back to complete the race. Can you finish it in 2 minutes?|
|Completing First Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: Not bad, [player]! [NPC] Gustave: I hope you can get a better than this! [NPC] Gustave: Here's a Polished Pebble. I found it laying around... [NPC] Gustave: Try again, but this time come back in 1 minute or less!|
|Before Completing Second Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: Keep it up! Can you finish the race in 1 minute or less?|
|Completing Second Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: You're getting much faster! [NPC] Gustave: Use my Hunter Knife. Holding it somehow makes me go faster. [NPC] Gustave: See if you can beat 32 seconds!|
|Before Completing Third Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: Keep at it! Can you finish the race in 32 seconds or less?|
|Completing Third Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: Now we're cooking! You're getting close to my record! [NPC] Gustave: Here's an trinket I've found in the caverns behind the waterfall. [NPC] Gustave: Finish the race in under 18 seconds and come talk to me!|
|Before Completing Fourth Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: Real challenge! Can you finish the race in 18 seconds or less and beat my personal record?|
|Completing Fourth Race ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: You did it! Congratulations, [player]. You are now faster than Gustave! [NPC] Gustave: I've been racing this island so fast that some bark from the trees snatched right off! [NPC] Gustave: Here's a rare piece of Silky Lichen. Maybe you can do something with it. [NPC] Gustave: I heard this lichen could improve some weapons!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; Completed all Races ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: You're always welcome to try the race again. Just run over the pressure plate to begin!|
|None; Completed all Races ([[Woods Race]])|[NPC] Gustave: It's a great day to go for a run!|
## Quest
The [[Woods Race]] is a quest in which the player must complete a race across various levels of [[The Park]] within various time frames to receive various rewards from Gustave.
## Trivia
- Gustave's skin may be a reference to the crocodile Gustave.

# Gwendolyn
Aliases: Crystal Hollows Pass, Crystal hollows pass
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines
[Gwendolyn]

Quests: We Need to Go Deeper ([[Quests]])

Gwendolyn is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Dwarven Mines]] who lets the player access the [[Crystal Hollows]] for 6 hours for 10,000 [[Coins]] if they have reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]].
After reaching [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] and helping [[Dulin]], the pass to the [[Crystal Hollows]] becomes free.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None; Not HotM Tier 4 ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|[NPC] Gwendolyn: Come back to me when you reach Heart of the Mountain Tier 4.|

[NPC] Gwendolyn: We dwarves have tried to setup outposts there but it is harder than it looks. [NPC] Gwendolyn: Nowadays this place is only used for expeditions and adventures! [NPC] Gwendolyn: A fantastic place to explore and mine Gemstones. [NPC] Gwendolyn: I'll grant you access to the Crystal Hollows for 6 hours, for only 10,000 coins! [NPC] Gwendolyn: Once access is granted, you can always come here by using the Fast Travel menu in your SkyBlock Menu! [NPC] Gwendolyn: You should also talk to Emissary Braum, they have more information about commissions.|
|Purchasing Pass|[NPC] Gwendolyn: Great! Now hop on into the Minecart and I'll get you on your way!|
|Asking A [[Minecart]] When The Pass Is Active|[NPC] Gwendolyn: Good luck in the mines!|
|Missing [[Minecart]]|[NPC] Gwendolyn: Oh no! It looks like you missed the Minecart!|
|After Reaching Heart of the Mountain VII (7) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|[NPC] Gwendolyn: Hey! Someone special has been asking to meet you! [NPC] Gwendolyn: I'm not quite sure what he's doing back in the Dwarven Mines- it's been years since I last saw him! [NPC] Gwendolyn: Anyways, he's expecting you over in the Hanging Court by the Royal Palace. Better not keep him waiting!|
|None; Before 0.12|[NPC] Gwendolyn: One day I will be useful.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Gwendolyn: I don't really have a sweet tooth, but chocolate is probably my favorite thing in this world. But the supply is really poor mostly because of the mines' lack of accessibility.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Gwendolyn: Amazing! I haven't eaten such chocolatey goodness in a long time...Maybe they should invest in that rusty old lift.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Gwendolyn: I helped you enter the Crystal Hollows so many times! Why won't you return the favor?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|

# Harriette
Categories: Non-player characters
[Harriette]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]

Harriette is an [[NPC]] who first appears in the [[Barry Center]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They start out as a protestor against [[Barry]], but after the player handles their complaints, they move to [[Barry HQ]] and become a worker.
## Dialougue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Harriette: Barry is a terrible leader! [NPC] Harriette: He doesn't want to hear us out!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Harriette: Barry is hiding from us! [NPC] Harriette: He doesn't want to face reality!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Harriette: Someone promised donuts when coming to this protest. [NPC] Harriette: And I don't see ANY DONUT! [NPC] Harriette: Screw Barry!|
|After first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Harriette: I read in the Rift Herald that Barry wants to make jumping illegal! [NPC] Harriette: That's not okay with me, I love jumping. [NPC] Harriette: When I wake up in the morning, I jump. [NPC] Harriette: If I'm feeling blue, I jump! [NPC] Harriette: Barry hates us all jumpers. I should tell Barry about Harriette's complaints...|
|After telling Barry about Harriette's complaints|[NPC] Harriette: Barry is an anti-jumper, a bigot and stands against all my values. Select the appropriate Barry rhetoric: ① [Barry said jumping is an affront to gravity.] ② [Barry said you shouldn't base your identity on jumping.] ③ [Barry said the Rift Herald is bought and paid for by the mob.]|
|Selecting ① [Barry said jumping is an affront to gravity.]|[NPC] Harriette: So what? [NPC] Harriette: The Rift Herald made me realize... [NPC] Harriette: What has gravity ever done for me?|
|Selecting ② [Barry said you shouldn't base your identity on jumping.]|[NPC] Harriette: And what do YOU base your identity on? [NPC] Harriette: Your cata level???|
|Selecting ③ [Barry said the Rift Herald is bought and paid for by the mob.]|[NPC] Harriette: Oh my god. [NPC] Harriette: They should say so on the cover!! [NPC] Harriette: Instead of blindly following a newspaper, I will instead follow Barry, a real politician of the people!|
|In [[Barry HQ]]|[NPC] Harriette: I'm working on a resolution to ban anti-jumping. [NPC] Harriette: If it works, everyone will stay midair all the time.|
## Trivia
- While in [[Barry Center]], Harriette and the other protestors have dialogue above their heads, such as "Barry bad!", "Go away Barry!", "Barry is trash.", "Go away Barry!", "Barry boooo!", and "Barry sucks!"

# Haymitch

Haymitch is an [[NPC]] located in the spawn area of the [[Spider's Den]]. They give tips for exploring the [[Spider's Den]] and what to expect.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Haymitch: There's lots to do in the Spider's Den. [NPC] Haymitch: Behind me is the Spiders Mound. [NPC] Haymitch: Be careful, though! The Broodmother has made the summit home, and she's quite tenacious!|
|Before having entered the [[Spider Mound]]|[NPC] Haymitch: Try to reach the top of the Spider Mound. [NPC] Haymitch: Once there, test yourself against the Broodmother, if you're up to the challenge! [NPC] Haymitch: Be careful, though. The Broodmother has made the summit to her home, and she's quite tenacious!|
|Before having entered the [[Spider Mound]]|[NPC] Haymitch: There's lots to do in the Spider's Den. [NPC] Haymitch: Behind me is Spider Mound. Many like to test their skills by trying to reach the top of it. [NPC] Haymitch: Be careful, though. The Broodmother has made the summit to her home, and she's quite tenacious!|
|After having entering the [[Spider Mound]]|[NPC] Haymitch: Wow you're quite strong!. [NPC] Haymitch: Unfortunately, there's always bigger fish. Or, in this case, a bigger spider. [NPC] Haymitch: But, for now, you should hone your skills until you reach Combat Skill Level 12, and then travel to The End.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Below Combat XII (12) ([[Combat]])|[NPC] Haymitch: Hone your skills until you reach Combat Skill Level 12, and then travel to The End!|
|Combat XII+ ([[Combat]])|[NPC] Haymitch: The gravel on this island seems to magically reappear when you break it. I don't ask questions.|
|Combat XII+ ([[Combat]])|[NPC] Haymitch: Those Dashing Spiders are pretty obnoxious. I find a well-placed arrow can help with that.|
|Combat XII+ ([[Combat]])|[NPC] Haymitch: Broodmother, Broodfather, Arachne... There are so many dangerous spiders around here.|
|Combat XII+ ([[Combat]])|[NPC] Haymitch: Slimes seem to spawn on the hills when it rains. They're a bit more dangerous during a thunderstorm.|

# Hina
Aliases: Hina's Chapters
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Hina]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Heart of the Forest ([[Quests]])
Description: Unlocks the [[Heart of the Forest]] and [[Chapters]] for the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
Location: [[Tangleburg's Path]]
Coordinates: -553.5 110 -16.5
Hina is an [[NPC]] located in [[Tangleburg's Path]]. They introduce the player to the [[Heart of the Forest]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Hina: Hola! [NPC] Hina: Welcome to Galatea! [NPC] Hina: I'm here to give you your own Heart of the Forest. [NPC] Hina: Everyone has one around here! [NPC] Hina: The Heart of the Forest can be found in your SkyBlock Menu while on Galatea. [NPC] Hina: You can unlock and level up various perks and abilities there. [NPC] Hina: You can talk to Agatha near the lake to gain HOTF Exp. [NPC] Hina: To level up your perks you will need Forest Whispers. These are obtained by cutting trees! HEART OF THE FOREST has been unlocked! Open your SkyBlock Menu to find it. [CLICK HERE TO OPEN] [NPC] Hina: I also have tasks for you to do while on this island. [NPC] Hina: For each task I will reward you handsomely!|
|After completing all of Hina's Chapters|[NPC] Hina: You've done what few even try. Each creature you met each tree you felled, it all shaped you. And now, you shape Galatea. [NPC] Hina: Look at you. Sweeping like a storm, hunting like a whisper. I couldn't be prouder. [NPC] Hina: You've mastered every path I know. [NPC] Hina: Congratulations [[Player]].|
## Hina's Chapters
Hina's Chapters are a form of progression that guides the player through [[Galatea]].
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
Begin your journey through the forest.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Talk to [[Swoop]]|[[Swoop]] will teach you the basics of [[Foraging]] on [[Galatea]].|+100 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])|
|Use the Fusion Machine ([[Attribute Fusion]])|The Fusion Machine ([[Attribute Fusion]]) will be extremely useful in your [[Hunting]] journey.|+100 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Talk to David ([[David Hunterborough]]) and his Hu ([[Oden]])nt ([[Jaeger]])ers ([[Auryon]])|David ([[David Hunterborough]])'s quest teaches you the ropes of [[Hunting]].|+100 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
[Chapter I Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter II; +200 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +1,500 Foraging XP (Foraging); +5 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels); 1x Phanpyre Shard (Attributes); 1x Cod Shard (Attributes); 1x Lapis Zombie Shard (Attributes)]
It's time to explore and face new creatures and challenges.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Obtain 10 Fig ([[Fig Log]]) [[Tree Gifts]]|Remember to start from the trunk!|+200 Forest Whispers ([[Forest Whispers]])|
|Participate in a Daily Agatha Contest ([[Agatha]])|[[Agatha]] will reward you every day for participating in her contests!|+100 Heart Of The Forest XP ([[Heart of the Forest]])|
|Kill Chillshots|Chillshots and Chillblades can be found in a hidden cavern under the Village.|+1,000 Combat XP ([[Combat]])|
|Catch Verdant Shards|Verdant are green tropical fishes found in lakes and rivers.|+500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Catch Azure Shards|Azure are rarer, blue variants of Tropical Fishes.|+500 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
[Chapter II Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter III; +500 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +5,000 Foraging XP (Foraging); +10 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels); 1 Prime Lushlilac Bonbon; +200 Forest Whispers (Forest Whispers)]
Galatea awards those who pay attention.:
|Task|Description|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|Obtain 100 Fig Tree Gifts|Best to find a friend to cut trees together!|+2,000 Forest Whispers ([[Forest Whispers]])|
|Reach UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) in Agatha's Contest|You can earn points from many activities in Galatea!|+2,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])|
|Hold a [[Fig Hew]]|You can find the recipe of the Fig Hew in the Fig Collection!|+2,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])|
|Collect Berries from Bushes|You might encouter creatures when collecting berries!|+500 Heart Of The Forest XP ([[Heart of the Forest]])|
|Obtain a Shard using a Lushlilac Bonbon.|Killing monsters while under the effect of a Salt can give you a chance at catching their Shard ([[Attributes]])!|+750 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Catch Mossybit Shards|Frogs jump around the center lake ([[Murkwater Loch]])!|+750 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Find something on the ground...|If something shines on the ground, click on it!|+1,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])|
[Chapter III Completion Rewards]
[Unlock Chapter IV; +1,000 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +10,000 Foraging XP (Foraging); +15 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels); 3x Mossybit Shard (Attributes); +1,000 Forest Whispers (Forest Whispers)]
The halfway point in your adventure.:

|Reach RARE ([[Rarity]]) in Agatha's Contest|Gather [[Sweep]] and [[Foraging Fortune]] to gain more points from Trees!|+3,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])|

|Fish up a Wetwing|They are so cute!|+2,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

|Catch Cod Shards|Cods faint when they spin enough!|+1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Catch Salmon Shards|Salmon can be found in the lake.|+1,000 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Tune a beacon|The beacon can be found near the old tower.|+200 Heart Of The Forest XP ([[Heart of the Forest]])|
|Level up a perk in Rosemary's Shop|Rosemary might have the best view of the island from up there.|+6,000 Forest Whispers ([[Forest Whispers]])|

[Unlock Chapter V; +2,500 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +25,000 Foraging XP (Foraging); +5,000 Hunting XP (Hunting); +20 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels); 3x Salmon Shard (Attributes); 3x Cod Shard (Attributes); +2,000 Forest Whispers (Forest Whispers)]
You're ready for complex hunts and hidden adventures. Only true foragers thrive this deep.:

|Fish a Flexbone in the Tomb Floodway|Treasure chances are increased in the Tomb Floodway.|+3,000 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

|Catch Coralot Shards|Axolotl get easily scared when you move.|+1,250 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Catch Dreadwing Shards|And those aren't even the largest Phantoms.|+1,250 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|Find a Mudworm|Lots of creatures live inside the ground.|+1,250 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|

[Unlock Chapter VI; +5,000 Heart Of The Forest XP (Heart of the Forest); +50,000 Foraging XP (Foraging); +7,500 Hunting XP (Hunting); +20 Forest Essence (Forest Essence); +25 SkyBlock XP (SkyBlock Levels); 3x Hideonleaf Shard (Attributes); 3x Coralot Shard (Attributes); 1x Sparrow Shard (Attributes); +3,000 Forest Whispers (Forest Whispers)]
It's time to go deeper. The old forest continues underwater.:

# Honey
Aliases: Honey Lathering, Tree Protection Agency, Tree Protection Order
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, More info needed
[Honey]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to the Tree Protection Agency and Honeycombs ([[Honeycomb]]).
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -627.5 99 226.5
Honey is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. She introduces the player to the Tree Protection Agency and [[Pots of Honeycomb]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Honey: Psst, have you heard of the Tree Protection Agency? Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Honey: Well, they're an evil group that is all about protecting the Trees! [NPC] Honey: Any poor tree with a Tree Protection Order on it, you simply can't cut! [NPC] Honey: Though, while we can't cut them, what we can do is deface them a little... Select an option: [Deface them?!]|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Honey: Well I'm gonna tell you anyways, because you don't look like someone who knows a lot. [NPC] Honey: So, about the Tree Protection Agency... [NPC] Honey: Well, they're an evil group that is all about protecting the Trees! [NPC] Honey: Any poor tree with a Tree Protection Order on it, you simply can't cut! [NPC] Honey: Though, while we can't cut them, what we can do is deface them a little... Select an option: [Deface them?!]|
|Selecting [Deface them?!]|[NPC] Honey: Yeah, if you progress through the Honeycomb Collection, you'll notice that there's a Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb. [NPC] Honey: If you make one of those, you can go right up to a Protected Tree and smear Honeycomb all over it! [NPC] Honey: Then, if you check back after a while, a Critter will have come along and started eating all the Honeycomb! [NPC] Honey: Eh, why don't I just show you a visual?|
## Tree Protection Order
Tree Protection Orders are restrictions placed on certain trees around [[Galatea]]. Attempting to cut them will result in a 1,000 [[Coins]] fine if the player has sufficient coins: instead, the player can lather them with [[Pots of Honeycomb]], spawning 1 of 3 exclusive [[Critters|Critter]] mobs.

# Hooded Occultist
Aliases: Occultist
Categories: Non-player characters
[Hooded Occultist]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Walks around a fountain.
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -689.5 17 232.5
The Hooded Occultists are four [[NPC|NPCs]] located in an underground cave in [[Torrhus Canyon]]. This cave can be accessed from [[Coordinates|-676, 90, 257]]. There is no known implementation of the NPCs or any information about their structure.
## Purpose
The four Hooded Occultists walk around a fountain and randomly switch walking clockwise or counterclockwise.
## Dialogue

# Hoppity
Categories: Non-player characters
[Hoppity]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Appears during [[Hoppity's Hunt]] to sell [[Chocolate Rabbits]].
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 56.5 70 -2.5
Hoppity is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]] that appears during [[Hoppity's Hunt]] event that sells [[Chocolate Rabbits]].
## Shop
Hoppity sells 7 random [[Chocolate Rabbits]] between [[Rarity|COMMON]]-[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity during [[Hoppity's Hunt]]. Their selection differs between players and is based on the [[Chocolate Factory]]'s prestige:
|Slot|Rabbit Rarity|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||100% (1 in 1)|
|2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])||100% (1 in 1)|
|3|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|90|90% (1 in 1.11)|
|3|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|10|10% (1 in 10)|
|4|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|75|75% (1 in 1.33)|
|4|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|25|25% (1 in 4)|
|5|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|50|50% (1 in 2)|
|5|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|40|40% (1 in 2.5)|
|5|RARE ([[Rarity]])|10|10% (1 in 10)|
|6|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|30|30% (1 in 3.33)|
|6|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50|50% (1 in 2)|
|6|RARE ([[Rarity]])|16|16% (1 in 6.25)|
|6|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|4|4% (1 in 25)|
|7|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10|10% (1 in 10)|
|7|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|45|45% (1 in 2.22)|
|7|RARE ([[Rarity]])|35|35% (1 in 2.86)|
|7|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|7|7% (1 in 14.29)|
|7|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|3|3% (1 in 33.33)|
Each rabbit can only be purchased once per season. [[Rarity|COMMON]] rabbits cost 200,000 [[Coins]], [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] rabbits cost 500,000 [[Coins]], [[Rarity|RARE]] rabbits cost 1,000,000 [[Coins]], [[Rarity|EPIC]] rabbits cost 2,500,000 [[Coins]], [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rabbits cost 5,000,000 [[Coins]]. If said rabbit has already been found before, purchasing the rabbit will grant [[Chocolate]] instead, with the amount depending on the rarity of the rabbit.
### Abiphone Contact
Once Hoppity's [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] has been acquired, Hoppity will randomly call the player (2% chance, rolled per [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]]), independent of whether [[Hoppity's Hunt]] is active. During this call Hoppity will offer to sell the player a [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] between [[Rarity|COMMON]]-[[Rarity|EPIC]] rarity:
|Rabbit|Weight|Chance|Chance (after acquiring Abi)|Cost|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Abi Rabbit|35|33.33% (1 in 3)||500,000 [[Coins]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|35|33.33% (1 in 3)|50.00% (1 in 2)|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|10|9.52% (1 in 10.5)|14.29% (1 in 7)|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|20|19.05% (1 in 5.25)|28.57% (1 in 3.5)|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5|4.76% (1 in 21)|7.14% (1 in 14)|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Initial Interaction|[NPC] Hoppity: Hello!!! [NPC] Hoppity: I'm the proud owner of the Chocolate Factory! [NPC] Hoppity: My Chocolate is the finest chocolate in the entire world. [NPC] Hoppity: I have a brand new marketing strategy, and every Spring season I will spread various Rabbit Eggs across the islands. [NPC] Hoppity: Collect all the Rabbits I left in those eggs and I will let you use my factory! [NPC] Hoppity: Happy egg hunting!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Hoppity: ✆ What? You want my Abiphone contact? [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ Well, tell you what. Help me out with a little something and I'll hook you up. [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ There's this one Fish that keeps munching on my chocolate stash. [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ He's a clever one, that fish. I can never catch him. [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ If I ever get him, I'm making a meal out of him - a little payback for all that chocolate. [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ Bring me a Fish Chocolat à la Vapeur and I'll give you my contact. Deal? [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Hoppity: ✆ Thank you so much, here is my contact. [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ If I have new rabbit arrivals, I'll let you know! ✆ Hoppity has been added to your Abiphone's contacts!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Not Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Hoppity: ✆ Bring me that Fish...but in steamed form!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Phone Call|✆ Hoppity ✆ ✆ RING... [PICK UP] ✆ RING... RING... [PICK UP] ✆ RING... RING... RING... [PICK UP] [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ What's up, [PLAYER]? [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ I just got a new Chocolate Rabbit and was wondering if you wanted to buy it. Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Accepting/Purchasing from Call Shop|[NPC] Hoppity: ✆ Nice doing business with you. [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ See you around!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Denying/Not Purchasing from Call Shop|[NPC] Hoppity: ✆ Alright, maybe next time. [NPC] Hoppity: ✆ See you around!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Hoppity: Hoppity's Hunt is on, and I need a break! Got something sweet?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Hoppity: Simply exquisite! I don't think I'll ever get tired of chocolate.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Hoppity: Oh...stingy, aren't we?|
## Bugs
- It is possible to obtain [[Chocolate Rabbits]] that the player has not met the requirement from via Hoppity's shop or [[Abiphones|Abiphone]].

# Hungry Hiker
Aliases: Hiker
Categories: Non-player characters, Farming, Quest giver
[Hungry Hiker]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Tasks the player with bringing him food.
Location: [[Mushroom Gorge]]
Coordinates: 269.5 48 -480.5
Description: Appears here after the player brings him food.
Location: [[Desert Settlement]]
Coordinates: 148.5 77 -374.5
The Hungry Hiker is an [[NPC]] that fell down the ravine at the [[Mushroom Gorge]]. Interacting with him will begin a quest in which he asks the player to bring him food. After the quest is finished, Hungry Hiker will tell the player to visit him again prior to 144 SkyBlock days (2 real-life days). The quest is completed after being fed 10 times without failing, and the player is rewarded with an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Farming Exp Boost]]. He can also offer the item as a reward for an offer in [[The Garden]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: Hello there, stranger! [NPC] Hungry Hiker: I fell down into this ravine a couple days ago and can't climb out. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: My friend Jake said he would come get me but he hasn't arrived yet. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: Could you bring me food until he gets here? [NPC] Hungry Hiker: Could you get me # of this food? [NPC] Hungry Hiker: The food I want is <food>.|
|Incorrect food|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: This isn't the food I wanted, please get me the correct food. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: I asked for food that is a <food>.|
|Incorrect amount|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: This isn't the correct amount I asked for. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: I asked for <amount> of this item.|
|Correct food and amount|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: Thanks for the food. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: This should fill me up for 144 SkyBlock days. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: Come back before then so I don't perish!|
|After giving food|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: I appreciate the effort but you recently gave me food. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: Come back later with more food!|
|Finished Quest ([[Quests]]) (giving food 10 times)|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: Thanks for the food! [NPC] Hungry Hiker: I think I see Jake on the edge of the ravine! [NPC] Hungry Hiker: Thank you for feeding me all these days, meet me at the house near the portal and I will give you something to show my gratitude!|
|After Quest ([[Quests]]) is completed, in his hut|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: Thank you for feeding me all those days. [NPC] Hungry Hiker: To show my gratitude, please have this!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: Hello there, I seem to be stuck here with no good food to eat, could you bring me some square orange fruits?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: Thanks so much for feeding me, please take this as a token of my gratitude.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Hungry Hiker: I guess I'll just go hungry then...|
## Quests
|Dialogue|Item|Amount|
|-|-|-|
|grilled meat|[[Cooked Porkchop]]|30x|
|red and crunchy|[[Apple]]|20x|
|made of wheat|[[Bread]]|25x|
|from a cow|[[Cooked Beef]]|30x|
|red on the inside and green on the outside|[[Melon Slice]]|64x|
|cooked potato|[[Baked Potato]]|30x|
|meat from a fowl|[[Cooked Chicken]]|30x|
## Trivia
- The Hungry Hiker is referenced in the [[Friendly Hiker]]'s dialogue.
- The Hungry Hiker will disappear if he is not fed within 144 SkyBlock days (2 real-life days).
  - If he disappears, you can start the quest again by talking to the [[Friendly Hiker]] after 5 real-life days.
- The player can feed the Hungry Hiker as early as 36 SkyBlock days (12 real-life hours) after he was last fed.
- After completing his quest, he moves back to his hut in the [[Desert Settlement]].
- The Hungry Hiker will not accept the given items if it's not exactly the requested amount, even if it's more than what was requested.
- The Hungry Hiker used to have several conditions where he would ask for food items that were unobtainable at the time; particularly, Rabbit Stew and 30x Cooked Rabbit. Both of these have since been patched.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added Hungry Hiker.|
|Unknown Date|Added the UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Farming Exp Boost]] as a reward. Note: It is unknown if this was in the game since the 0.11.4 update (on April 21st, 2021), or if it was added later.|
|Jun 22, 2022|Changed Rabbit Stew requirement to 30 [[Cooked Chicken]].|

# Hypixel Discord
Aliases: Discord (NPC), Hypixel Discord (NPC)
Categories: Non-player characters
  This page is about an [[NPC]]. [[Hypixel|Hypixel's]] official Discord is available at hypixel.net/discord.
[Hypixel Discord]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about the official Hypixel Discord server.
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 10.5 71 -15.5
Hypixel Discord is an [[NPC]] in the [[Village]] that talks about the official Hypixel Discord server.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before account linking|Link your Discord account to receive unique rewards: • Discord Approved Achievement • SkyBlock - Discord Emblem • Lobbies - Ping Click Effect Click here to link your Hypixel account to our Discord!|
|After account linking|Thank you for joining and linking to our Hypixel Discord! https://discord.gg/hypixel You received: • Discord Approved Achievement • SkyBlock - Discord Emblem • Lobbies - Ping Click Effect|
## Trivia
- Below Hypixel Discord's nametag is text that says CLICK TO JOIN!.
- The player receives an ⌬ [[SkyBlock Levels|Emblem]] as a reward for connecting a Discord account to the [[Hypixel|Hypixel Network]].
  - This is most likely a reference to the Server Boost Discord icon.
- Their mask references the Discord logo. The character on the hoodie is Wumpus, Discord's mascot.
- This [[NPC]] was positioned where [[Security Sloth]] used to be, who was moved into the [[Security Hall]] a few days later.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 16, 2026|Added Hypixel Discord.|

# Igrupan's Chickens
Categories: Non-player characters
[Igrupan's Chickens]
Type: [[NPC]]
[[Igrupan|Igrupan's]] chickens are 5 chickens located in Scarleton on the Crimson Isle. During the Mage Faction quest line, the chickens escape their coop and [[Igrupan]] will ask the player to bring back all his missing chickens to [[Igrupan's Chicken Coop]].
These are their locations:
- Henrietta : [[Coordinates|-9, 92, -848]]
- Chikira : [[Coordinates|-48, 112, -857]]
- Chickovsky : [[Coordinates|-24, 116, -813]]
- Cluckles : [[Coordinates|-49, 103, -825]]
- Hennifer : [[Coordinates|-63, 121, -818]]
The chickens have to be lured back, either by punching them, attacking them with a weapon with [[Knockback (Enchantment)|Knockback]], with [[Seeds]], or with a [[Lead]].

# Ike
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Ike]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Rewards the player for killing different [[Mob Types]].
Location: [[Michael's Crib]]
Coordinates: -260.5 94.5 -284.5
Ike is an [[NPC]] located in [[Michael's Crib]] on the [[Spider's Den]]. He introduces the player to [[Mob Types]].
## Purpose
Ike tracks which [[Mob Types]] the player has killed. Killing a new type for the first time unlocks a claimable reward, including +1,000 [[Combat|Combat XP]], 1,000 [[Coins]], and [[SkyBlock Levels|1 SkyBlock XP]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Ike: You heard what Michael said about Mob Types? Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Ike: Not to flex, but I've seen enough films to know about every mob type in existence. [NPC] Ike: I'm bored, so let's play a game, just us two. Select an option: [What kind of game?]|
|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Ike: Then go talk to him, bro.|
|Selecting [What kind of game?]|[NPC] Ike: You go out and slay some mobs and, every time you kill a mob with a new mob type, I'll add it to my book! [NPC] Ike: I'll give you a reward each time you kill a new mob type, so get busy! [NPC] Ike: Talk to me again if you want to check your progress!|
|Subsequent interactions|[NPC] Ike: Dude, I'm so bored out here. Mind showing me what you've been up to?|
|Subsequent interactions|[NPC] Ike: 'Sup champ! How's that Mob Type collection coming along? Let's take a look!|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 31, 2025|Added Ike.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Added Ike.|
Linked from: [[Locations]], [[Locations/Spider's Den]], [[Michael's Crib]], [[Mob Types]], [[NPC/List/Spider's Den]], [[Spider's Den]], [[Stranded]]

# Ikrus
Categories: Non-player characters
[Ikrus]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tries to jump to the sun, but dies in the process.
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: 9.5 157 60.5
Ikrus is an [[NPC]] located in [[The Mountaintop]]. Helping him try and reach the sun rewards [[Burned Pants]] and an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Ikrus: That's it, I'm about to make it out of here! [NPC] Ikrus: You see the sun way up there in the sky? Select an option: [No I don't, there is no Sun in here...]|
|Selecting [No I don't, there's no Sun in here...]|[NPC] Ikrus: If I can reach it, I'll be able to exit this place- I'm sure of it! [NPC] Ikrus: Anyway, I've been prepping my super jump since I was a wee tot. [NPC] Ikrus: The more I squat before a jump, the higher I jump! [NPC] Ikrus: Gotta say, it's pretty far up though... [NPC] Ikrus: How many squats do you reckon I need? Like 3, 6, or maybe 9? Select an option: [3] [6] [9]|
|Selecting [3], [6], or [9]|[NPC] Ikrus: Hmmm, [#], huh? Sounds like a plan! [NPC] Ikrus: Here goes nothing!|
|After landing|[NPC] Ikrus: Woah, the exit is way higher than I thought... [NPC] Ikrus: Let's AMP IT UP a bit! What do you say to 12, 18, or even 24 squats? Select an option: [12] [18] [24]|
|Selecting [12], [18], or [24]|[NPC] Ikrus: Alright, deep breaths! Let's do this!|
|After landing|[NPC] Ikrus: Ouch, that was a bit too much oomph. [NPC] Ikrus: But I felt super close that time! [NPC] Ikrus: The next one's gonna nail it. [NPC] Ikrus: I don't want to overshoot it though... [NPC] Ikrus: We need the perfect amount of squats. [NPC] Ikrus: What do you think? [NPC] Ikrus: 30, 40, or should we go big with 50? Select an option: [30] [40] [50]|
|Selecting [40] or [50]; (Rewards [[Burned Pants]])|[NPC] Ikrus: Hmm, [#], huh? Sounds like a plan! [NPC] Ikrus: Here goes nothing! Well, I hope he found what he was looking for...|
|Landing after selecting [30]|[NPC] Ikrus: YOWZA!!! [NPC] Ikrus: I was like, super-duper close this time! [NPC] Ikrus: C'mon, what's next? Maybe 40 or do we just go wild and try 50? Select an option: [40] [50]|
|Selecting [40] or [50]; (Rewards [[Burned Pants]])|[NPC] Ikrus: Okay, it's showtime! 40 squats here we come... [NPC] Ikrus: Let's make this jump legendary! Well, I hope he found what he was looking for...|
## Trivia
- Ikrus may be a reference to Icarus from Greek mythology. In the myth, Icarus ignored his father Daedalus' instructions not to fly too close to the Sun, causing the beeswax in his wings to melt. Icarus fell from the sky, plunged into the sea, and drowned.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Ikrus.|
Linked from: [[Achievements]], [[Burned Pants]], [[Enigma Souls]], [[Enigma Souls/List]], [[Locations]], [[Locations/Rift Dimension]], [[NPC/List/Rift]], [[Rift Dimension]], [[The Mountaintop]]

# Incredible Raffle Box
Aliases: Century Raffle Box
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Incredible Raffle Box]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Opens the Raffle ([[Raffle of the Century]]) menu.
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -10 76 3
The Incredible Raffle Box is a raffle box located in the [[Village]] for the [[Raffle of the Century]] event.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before the event starts|[NPC] Raffle Box: Hey it's me, the raffle box. [NPC] Raffle Box: The event hasn't started yet. [NPC] Raffle Box: Don't tell anyone I can talk.|
|After the event starts, before speaking to Simon or Rezzus|[NPC] Raffle Box: Hey I'm just a box, I can't explain the event! [NPC] Raffle Box: Talk with Simon or Rezzus first!|
## Mechanics
The Raffle Box has 3 ongoing Raffles known as Speed Raffle, Daily Raffle and The Big One, the speed raffle rolls every 2 hours, the daily raffle every 24 hours and the big one at the end of the event.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 31, 2023|Added the Incredible Raffle Box.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 2, 2023|Added the Incredible Raffle Box.|
|Jan 28, 2025|Changed how Raffle Rewards are given.|
|Jan 28, 2025|Added various new items to the raffle.|

# Inverted Sirius
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Rift Dimension
[Inverted Sirius]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to transfer # Rift-Transferable # ([[Rift Dimension]]) items to outside the dimension.
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -96.5 75 190.5
Inverted Sirius is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wyld Woods]]. After the player gives them the [[Stability Elixir]], they allow [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]] items to be transferred from the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] to the main dimension and grant the [[Doubloon of the Family]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: Howdy! [NPC] Inverted Sirius: As you see, I've done all the legwork... all the research! [NPC] Inverted Sirius: I found a way to smuggle items in and out of the Rift Dimension. [NPC] Inverted Sirius: Bring me a Stability Elixir and I'll show you how!|
|Before giving [[Stability Elixir]]|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: Bring me a Stability Elixir and I'll let you smuggle items between dimensions! [NPC] Inverted Sirius: You should be able to find the potion at any local brewery.|
|Opening [[Ender Chest]]; Haven't Given Stability Elixir|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: Woah there! [NPC] Inverted Sirius: Don't touch that!|
|Giving [[Stability Elixir]]|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: That hits the spot! [NPC] Inverted Sirius: Alright, both here and in the real world I've placed this Ender Chest. [NPC] Inverted Sirius: Use it to transfer Rift-Transferable items. [NPC] Inverted Sirius: Keep in mind, the items may turn different between dimensions! [NPC] Inverted Sirius: Here, take this example item.|
|Not Claiming [[Doubloon of the Family]]|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: Uh... you didn't pick up the example item.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: You know, you learn a lot from looking at people's shoes.|
|None|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: I don't know how long I've been stuck here. [NPC] Inverted Sirius: Smuggling items is easy, but transferring people has its dangers.|
|None|[NPC] Inverted Sirius: Don't tell the wizard about me.|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Inverted Sirius.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Inverted Sirius.|

# Jaeger
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Jaeger]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Teaches the player how to use a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).
Location: [[Murkwater Loch]]
Coordinates: -651.5 89 -24.5
Jaeger is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Murkwater Loch]] on [[Galatea]] that teaches the player how to use a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before unlocking [[Heart of the Forest]]|[NPC] Jaeger: <player>! Have you talked to Hina at the front of the island to get your hands on a Heart of the Forest?|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Jaeger: Hey, are you David Hunterborough's new apprentice? [NPC] Jaeger: I've been hunting fish all day long, why don't you give it a try? [NPC] Jaeger: But first grab this fishing net.|
|After accepting the [[Basic Fishing Net]]|[NPC] Jaeger: I'm guessing you have never used a fishing net before? [NPC] Jaeger: All you have to do is throw it at fish you see in the water. [NPC] Jaeger: Try it on this fish right here.|
|Before using the [[Basic Fishing Net]]|[NPC] Jaeger: All you have to do is throw it at any fish you see in the water! [NPC] Jaeger: Try it on this fish right there.|
|After using the [[Basic Fishing Net]]|[NPC] Jaeger: Well done! [NPC] Jaeger: You've successfully ended the peaceful existence of a fish! [NPC] Jaeger: That's what all hunters live for! [NPC] Jaeger: David Hunterborough's going to be over the moon about this. [NPC] Jaeger: Make sure to share every gripping detail of your epic conquest with him next.|
|Before talking to [[David Hunterborough]]|[NPC] Jaeger: You should go talk to David Hunterborough next.|
|After talking to [[David Hunterborough]]|[NPC] Jaeger: Those tiny fish are super cute, that's mostly why I hunt them. [NPC] Jaeger: The creatures deep down this loch are quite scary.|
